# EduNexus Implementation Log

A durable, append-only record of what actually got built, sprint by sprint. This complements the architecture documents in `docs/architecture/` without turning them into changelogs — those documents describe the *permanent target state and rules*; this log describes *what happened, when, and why*, in Phase B's disciplined, small-commit execution mode.

Every completed sprint, stage, or significant change gets one entry, in this format, newest first:

```
## YYYY-MM-DD — <Sprint/Stage name>

**What changed**: one or two sentences, plain language.

**Architectural documents referenced**: which of the Constitution / RAS / Canonical Domain Registry /
Deprecation Registry / Evolution Blueprint / Execution Plan / Stage 0 / Stage 0.5 governed this change.

**ADR**: link to `docs/architecture/adr/NNNN-title.md`, or "None — no trigger condition met."

**Tests added**: what, and where.

**Rollback considerations**: what it takes to undo this, if anything.
```

Entries are never edited to rewrite history — if a change is later reverted or corrected, a new entry records that, referencing the original.

---

## 2026-07-18 — Sprint 12Z: Learner Projects Domain Foundation (Canonical Implementation)

**What changed**: implemented the first Learner Projects domain per ADR-0013, in a Guardian Architect role optimizing for architectural correctness over speed. Re-ran the mandatory Phase 1 audit against the current repo state (re-reading `lib/learnerPortfolio/` in full as the mission specifically instructed) — confirmed ADR-0013's own conclusion still held: no canonical Projects domain existed. Built: migration `20260718100000_learner_projects.sql` — five tables (`learner_projects`, `project_members`, `project_mentors`, `project_updates`, `project_artifacts`) plus one additive nullable `project_id` column on `portfolio_items` — applied only after explicit user approval, per this sprint's own verification checklist (a deliberate departure from Sprints 12V/12W's practice of applying directly, followed exactly as instructed); `ProjectRepository` (`repos.projects`, deliberately named and commented to distinguish it from the pre-existing unrelated `repos.projections`), one named method per lifecycle transition, no generic update/delete; `lib/learnerProjects/project.ts` — ADR-0013's actual frozen ten-state lifecycle (Draft/Planning/In Progress/Submitted/Reviewed/Verified/Published/Archived/Rejected/Cancelled), not the mission brief's own simplified five-state restatement, per the brief's own "must comply with ADR-0013, no exceptions" instruction; `composeProjects()` wired into `composeBlueprint.ts`, summary-only (count/latest-published/current-active/featured/URL, proven by an explicit key-set test assertion to never leak internal lifecycle state); the Portfolio-references-Projects relationship (`resolveProjectReference()` in the new `lib/learnerPortfolio/portfolioProjectLink.ts` — `linked`/`reserved`/`not_applicable`, never a fabricated reference, all three states proven by tests). One deliberate, ADR-grounded difference from Achievement, proven by a passing test rather than just asserted: Evidence is optional at `verifyProject()` (ADR-0013 Phase 7 says "may reference," not Achievement's own non-negotiable rule). Achievement's relationship to Projects was verified by reading `lib/learnerAchievement/` in full, not built — the mission's own Forbidden list bans "Achievement redesign," so no schema link was added there this sprint. Documented three honest Known Gaps rather than silently deferring them: no learner/parent-facing access path exists yet (matches Portfolio/Achievement's identical posture at their own foundation sprints), no curation/pinning mechanism for "featured," and Competition/External verification's exact data model remains reserved for a future sprint.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0013-learner-projects-domain.md` (governs the whole domain — lifecycle, ownership matrix, categories, verification types, the frozen "Relationship to ADR-0011" resolution this sprint executed), `adr-0011-learner-portfolio-architecture.md` (the Portfolio side of the reference relationship), `adr-0012-learner-achievement-domain.md` (the Evidence-optional-vs-mandatory contrast, verified by direct comparison), CLAUDE.md (evidence-lifecycle mutation discipline, applied here to Projects).

**ADR**: none new — this sprint implements ADR-0013 as written; no trigger condition was met beyond what that ADR already covers.

**Tests added**: `lib/learnerProjects/project.integration.test.ts` — 8 tests against real synthetic Supabase data: full lifecycle with service-layer AND DB-trigger immutability, reject-vs-cancel as distinct terminal states with genuinely different reachability rules (reject from Submitted/Reviewed only, cancel from Draft/Planning/In Progress only, never after Submitted), canonical category enforcement, Blueprint composition (asserting the exact 5-field budget), the full Portfolio-references-Projects relationship, cross-school isolation, and permission checks. Also updated the two existing fixture-based pure tests (`composeBlueprint.pure.test.ts`, `lib/parentExperience/growthTimeline.pure.test.ts`) for the new required `LearnerBlueprint.projects` field. All pass; full regression scope (Portfolio + Achievement + Projects + Blueprint/snapshot/growth-timeline/parent-actions suites) re-run and passing.

**Rollback considerations**: schema is purely additive (`DROP TABLE learner_projects, project_members, project_mentors, project_updates, project_artifacts CASCADE` plus `ALTER TABLE portfolio_items DROP COLUMN project_id` fully reverses it, zero existing tables' own columns modified besides that one addition); `composeBlueprint.ts`'s new section degrades to `unavailable` on any failure without affecting sibling sections, so it can be removed by reverting the one integration commit with no data loss. No UI, uploads, Competitions, Innovation domain, Community Service, or Sprint 13 work exists yet — per the sprint's own Stop Condition, and per the user's own recommendation, the next step before any Sprint 13 domain work is a full architecture audit of Sprints 12A–12Z checking for duplicated ownership or diverging calculations across Blueprint/Portfolio/Achievement/Projects.

## 2026-07-18 — Sprint 12Y: Learner Projects Domain (Canonical Foundation, Architecture Only)

**What changed**: architecture and documentation only, per explicit mission instruction — no table, migration, repository, service, route, UI, media, upload, QR, or integration code was written. Audited the entire codebase first: found the sharpest naming collision this ADR series has produced — `lib/learnerPortfolio/` already has a Portfolio category literally named `projects`, but read closely it is a free-text-ish artefact label with no goal/lifecycle/team/mentor/verification, not a project-tracking system; also found and dismissed a pure vocabulary collision (`lib/projection/`/`learner_projections`, the unrelated Learner Intelligence computed-state engine — "Projection" shares five letters with "Projects" and nothing else) and read `assignments`/`assignment_submissions`/`lib/assignments/evidence.ts` in full (legacy scored classwork whose own code comments explicitly confirm "project work" was considered and rejected as an Evidence-producer candidate for lack of a real capture feature — confirming from the inside that no project-tracking capability exists anywhere). `docs/architecture/adr-0013-learner-projects-domain.md` freezes: Phase 2 (Projects is the work; Portfolio is the shelf; Achievement is the medal — three sentences that anchor every ownership decision after), Phase 3 (a full ownership matrix — Projects owns title/description/goal/category/status/dates/artifacts/team/reflection/mentor/verification; Portfolio owns only curation; Achievement owns only recognition; Blueprint/Career/Compass read only), Phase 4 (sixteen frozen categories, closed enum, extended only by a future named ADR amendment — explicitly distinguishing Project-category Leadership/Entrepreneurship from Achievement's own same-named values, never to be conflated), Phase 5 (a nine-state lifecycle — Draft/Planning/In Progress/Submitted/Reviewed/Verified/Published/Archived/Rejected/Cancelled — with Reviewed kept explicitly distinct from Verified since formative feedback and factual verification are different acts with different authority requirements, and Cancelled never reachable after Submitted since a submitted claim gets a real disposition, never a silent withdrawal), Phase 6 (six named verification types — Teacher/School/Competition/External/Self-only/Pending — no numeric trust score per Constitution Article XI, "Expired" explicitly rejected as a stored state matching Achievement's identical precedent, and the frozen rule that Projects owns verification permanently, Blueprint never computing or re-deriving trust independently), Phase 7 (six one-directional relationship arrows, ownership never reversing), Phase 8 (paper-summary/digital-everything/QR-future-only, extending ADR-0011's already-frozen split), Phase 9 (full Constitutional/RAS/ADR-0003-through-0012 compliance review, zero conflicts, plus an explicit "Relationship to ADR-0011" section that touches but does not supersede Portfolio's `projects` category — freezing now, for a future implementation sprint to execute rather than re-litigate, that a Portfolio entry under that category should eventually reference a Project entity rather than standing alone), Phase 10 (ten risks, each tied to the specific rule that prevents or defers it, including two risks — external-verification abuse and storage/media lifecycle — explicitly flagged as unsolved-by-this-ADR-alone rather than silently assumed safe).

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0013-learner-projects-domain.md` (new), `adr-0011-learner-portfolio-architecture.md` (touched, not superseded — the `projects` category disambiguation), `adr-0012-learner-achievement-domain.md` (the identical reference-not-copy/verification-owns-trust discipline applied one layer earlier), `adr-0003`/`adr-0004` (derived-data precedent for rejecting stored Expired states, applied to a third and fourth domain), `adr-0005` through `adr-0010` (ownership/lifecycle/QR/parent-visibility discipline, all extended not reinterpreted), `reference-architecture-specification.md` §3/§10, Educational Constitution Articles I/II/VI/IX/XI.

**ADR**: `docs/architecture/adr-0013-learner-projects-domain.md` — new, required because it introduces a new canonical domain (an ADR-trigger condition under Guardian mode).

**Tests added**: none — no code was written this sprint.

**Rollback considerations**: none — this sprint produced only two documents (the ADR and the sprint doc) plus this log entry. Fully reversible by definition. The one durable effect is documentation-level: any future Learner Projects implementation sprint (Sprint 12Z, per the user's own named roadmap) must read ADR-0013 before writing a single line of code, and must resolve the frozen Portfolio-`projects`-category reference-link as part of that sprint's own scope, not invent a different resolution under schedule pressure.

## 2026-07-18 — Sprint 12W: Learner Achievement Foundation

**What changed**: implemented the canonical Learner Achievement Domain exactly as frozen in ADR-0012 (flipped from Approved to Implemented as part of this sprint). Phase 1 audit (delegated, re-verified against current repo state including Sprint 12V's new Portfolio domain) re-confirmed ADR-0012's own findings still hold: no achievement/award/certificate/competition/leadership/badge/recognition data concept exists anywhere in the codebase — the only near-hits (Academy's teacher badge system, gamified study groups, career-advice prose) are all confirmed unrelated. Built the canonical domain: migration `20260718090000_learner_achievement.sql` (`learner_achievements`, `achievement_media`, `achievement_tags`, `achievement_verification_history`, RLS school-isolation policies, an immutability trigger enforcing ADR-0012's frozen six-state lifecycle — `rejected`/`revoked`/`archived` fully terminal, `published` permitting only the two named transitions to `archived` or `revoked`, DELETE legal only while `draft` — plus a second trigger making the verification-history table itself append-only); `AchievementRepository` (table access only, one named method per lifecycle transition); `lib/learnerAchievement/` service (the exact ADR-0012 lifecycle — Draft/Verified/Published/Rejected/Revoked/Archived, deliberately with no separate "Submitted" state since the ADR's own Phase 4 never named one; Achievement Type and Category kept as two separate frozen fields per Phase 2/Phase 8, never conflated; Phase 5's mandatory Evidence-or-verifying-document-reference rule enforced before `verifyAchievement()` succeeds; monotonic version counter plus append-only verification history on every transition); `composeAchievement()` wired into `composeBlueprint.ts` as a new always-optional section, summary-only (count, latest verified, highest-level via a fixed documented category ranking, profile URL), matching `composePortfolio`/`composeCareer`'s "ask, never compute" pattern exactly. One deliberate deviation from the sprint brief, resolved per the brief's own explicit "implement exactly as frozen in ADR-0012" instruction rather than asked about: the brief's Phase 5 category list and 5-state lifecycle diagram both conflict with ADR-0012's actual frozen Phase 2/Phase 8 type-vs-category split and six-state lifecycle — implemented the ADR's version, not the brief's simplified one, and documented the reasoning in the sprint doc. Verified explicitly: Portfolio (Sprint 12V) still owns zero achievement-related fields or categories; Blueprint composes both Portfolio and Achievement without owning either; Report Cards/Career Intelligence were not touched (no new read path added to either this sprint, matching the Forbidden list).

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0012-learner-achievement-domain.md` (governs the whole domain — now flipped to Implemented), `adr-0011-learner-portfolio-architecture.md` (the "compose, never own" precedent this ADR applies one layer earlier, and the domain whose non-duplication this sprint explicitly re-verified), `adr-0004-attendance-integration-principles.md` (the derived-data precedent for `expires_at` never being a stored status), CLAUDE.md (evidence-lifecycle mutation discipline, applied here to Achievement rows and to the verification-history audit trail).

**ADR**: none new — this sprint implements ADR-0012 as written; no trigger condition was met beyond what that ADR already covers.

**Tests added**: `lib/learnerAchievement/achievement.integration.test.ts` — 7 tests against real synthetic Supabase data: full lifecycle with service-layer AND DB-trigger immutability (including append-only verification history rows), the central Phase 5 evidence-or-document rule (both the rejection case and the "document reference alone is sufficient" case), reject-vs-revoke as distinct terminal states plus revocation removing external visibility even after publication, canonical type/category enforcement, Blueprint composition (empty + count/latest/highest-level summary, asserting the exact 4-field budget), cross-school isolation, and permission checks. Also updated the two existing fixture-based pure tests (`composeBlueprint.pure.test.ts`, `lib/parentExperience/growthTimeline.pure.test.ts`) for the new required `LearnerBlueprint.achievement` field. All pass.

**Rollback considerations**: schema is purely additive (`DROP TABLE learner_achievements, achievement_media, achievement_tags, achievement_verification_history CASCADE` fully reverses it, zero existing tables touched); `composeBlueprint.ts`'s new section degrades to `unavailable` on any failure without affecting sibling sections, so it can be removed by reverting the one integration commit with no data loss. No UI, uploads, certificates, badges, Career integration, or notifications exist yet — per the sprint's own Stop Condition, Sprint 12X and any Achievement UI/uploads/certificates/gamification work await separate approval.

**What changed**: implemented the first Learner Portfolio implementation sprint against ADR-0011/ADR-0012 (both flipped from DRAFT to Approved as part of this sprint). Phase 1 audit (delegated, verified against current repo state) re-confirmed both ADRs' own findings still hold: no learner-facing Portfolio/Achievement domain, upload pipeline, or storage bucket exists anywhere in the codebase. Built the canonical domain: migration `20260717160000_learner_portfolio.sql` (`learner_portfolios`, `portfolio_items`, `portfolio_media`, `portfolio_tags`, RLS school-isolation policies, a publish-immutability trigger that allows exactly one legal transition — Published → Archived — and rejects every other edit/delete on a published or archived row); `PortfolioRepository` (table access only, one named method per lifecycle transition, no generic update/delete); `lib/learnerPortfolio/` service (Draft → Submitted → Verified → Published → Archived lifecycle with Rejected reachable from Submitted, teacher-only verification workflow, field validation, canonical category enum deliberately excluding every ADR-0012 Achievement-owned concept — Awards/Certificates/Leadership/Competitions/Community Service/Innovation); `composePortfolio()` wired into `composeBlueprint.ts` as a new always-optional section, summary-only (published count + latest/featured highlight + URL), same "ask, never compute" pattern as `composeCareer`. Parent Portal/other Blueprint consumers automatically gain the new section with no code change (they already read `composeBlueprint()`'s output), satisfying the "Parent Portal reads Portfolio only through Blueprint" requirement structurally rather than via new integration code.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0011-learner-portfolio-architecture.md` (governs the whole domain — ownership, Blueprint relationship, Evidence reference-not-copy rule, paper/digital split), `docs/architecture/adr-0012-learner-achievement-domain.md` (governs the category exclusions), `adr-0005-learner-blueprint-architecture.md` §3, `adr-0008-blueprint-lifecycle-and-rendering.md` (immutability/snapshot precedent), CLAUDE.md (evidence-lifecycle mutation discipline, applied here to Portfolio items).

**ADR**: none new — this sprint implements ADR-0011/0012 as written; no trigger condition (new canonical domain, changed ownership, new architectural layer) was met beyond what those ADRs already cover.

**Tests added**: `lib/learnerPortfolio/portfolio.integration.test.ts` — 7 tests against real synthetic Supabase data: full lifecycle with both service-layer and DB-trigger immutability enforcement (including DELETE on published/archived rows), teacher-reject workflow, canonical-category enforcement (explicitly proving "innovation"/"awards" are rejected), mixed-category multi-item listing, Blueprint composition (empty-portfolio unavailable + published-summary available, asserting the exact 4-field budget), cross-school isolation, and permission checks. Also updated two existing fixture-based pure tests (`composeBlueprint.pure.test.ts`, `lib/parentExperience/growthTimeline.pure.test.ts`) for the new required `LearnerBlueprint.portfolio` field. All pass.

**Rollback considerations**: schema is purely additive (`DROP TABLE learner_portfolios, portfolio_items, portfolio_media, portfolio_tags CASCADE` fully reverses it, zero existing tables touched); `composeBlueprint.ts`'s new section degrades to `unavailable` on any failure without affecting other sections, matching every existing composer's contract, so it can be removed by reverting the one integration commit with no data loss. No UI, routes, or upload capability exist yet — per the sprint's own Stop Condition, Sprint 12W (Achievement) and any Portfolio UI/uploads/QR/AI work await separate approval.

## 2026-07-17 — Sprint 12U: Learner Achievement Domain (ADR-0012)

**What changed**: architecture and documentation only, per explicit mission instruction — no code, table, migration, repository, service, route, or UI was written, and no existing Blueprint/Portfolio/Parent Portal/Career/Compass/Evidence/Report Card/Teacher Reflection/Snapshot code was touched. Audited the entire codebase first, reading implementations rather than trusting filenames as explicitly instructed: re-confirmed `lib/academy/portfolio.ts` is EduNexus Academy's unrelated teacher professional-development badge system (already flagged once in ADR-0011's own audit), found `app/student/groups`/`app/dashboard/groups` to be an unrelated gamified study-group feature, and confirmed `components/organizations/sandbox-badge.tsx` is a static UI label with no data concept — no migration defines any achievements/awards/certificates/competitions/leadership table. Answer to the mission's explicit audit question: **neither one canonical achievement system nor several unrelated ones exist — zero exist.** `docs/architecture/adr-0012-learner-achievement-domain.md` freezes: Phase 2 (eleven domain terms defined, including two deliberate rejections — "Milestone" rejected entirely as already owned by Blueprint Snapshots/Growth Journey, and "Portfolio Item" defined only to state Achievement doesn't use it), Phase 3 (an ownership matrix plus an **explicit, named, reasoned partial supersession of ADR-0011 Phase 3** — Competitions/Leadership/Community Service/Innovation/Certifications/Awards/Recommendations move from Portfolio-owned to Achievement-owned, since ADR-0011 could only provisionally assign them before this domain existed; Portfolio's raw Creative Work artefact/Digital Artefacts/Reflection/Future Goals rows are unaffected), Phase 4 (a six-state lifecycle — Draft/Verified/Published/Rejected/Revoked/Archived — with "Expired" explicitly rejected as a stored state, since an expiry date is a computed, read-time fact, the same derived-vs-stored discipline ADR-0004 already established for Attendance, applied to a new domain for the first time), Phase 5 (the domain's central, non-negotiable rule: Evidence → Achievement, never the reverse, and never optional — stricter than Portfolio's own Evidence rule since Achievement makes a verifiable factual claim), Phase 6 (Portfolio composes Achievement, owns nothing of it — the identical rule Blueprint already follows toward everything it composes), Phase 7 (a full Blueprint-relationship table across Paper/Digital/QR/Snapshot/Parent Portal/University/Employer/Teacher — summary-only everywhere except Achievement's own future full-detail surface), Phase 8 (ten categories frozen, three rejected with reasoning — Citizenship and Environmental folded into Community Service, Culture folded into Creative Arts, each because it would only differ from its parent by topic/framing, never by anything the data model needs to treat differently), Phase 9 (a required-field verification model — who recorded, who verified, evidence, school, date — explicitly rejecting a numeric confidence field in favor of the lifecycle state itself carrying that meaning, per Constitution Article XI), Phase 10 (a four-state-by-six-audience visibility matrix, governed by one rule: nothing external before Published, nothing revoked ever shown externally), Phase 11 (seven risks, each tied to the specific rule that prevents it), Phase 12 (zero unresolved conflicts against the Constitution, RAS §3/§9/§10, ADR-0003/0004/0005/0010, and the one documented ADR-0011 supersession).

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0011-learner-portfolio-architecture.md` (partially, explicitly superseded per Phase 3), `adr-0005-learner-blueprint-architecture.md` §3, `adr-0010-parent-experience-architecture.md`, `adr-0004-attendance-integration-principles.md` (the derived-data precedent for rejecting "Expired" as a state), `adr-0003-attendance-domain.md`, `reference-architecture-specification.md` §3/§9/§10, Educational Constitution Articles I/II/VI/IX/XI.

**ADR**: `docs/architecture/adr-0012-learner-achievement-domain.md` — new, required both because it introduces a new canonical domain and because it explicitly, partially supersedes ADR-0011's own frozen ownership assignments (both are named ADR-trigger conditions under Guardian mode).

**Tests added**: none — no code was written this sprint.

**Rollback considerations**: none — this sprint produced only two documents (the ADR and the sprint doc) plus this log entry. Fully reversible by definition. The one durable effect is documentation-level: any future Portfolio implementation sprint must read ADR-0012 alongside ADR-0011, since ADR-0011 alone no longer accurately describes final ownership for the superseded rows.

## 2026-07-17 — Sprint 12T: Learner Portfolio Architecture (ADR-0011)

**What changed**: architecture and documentation only, per explicit mission instruction — no code, table, migration, repository, service, route, or UI was written. Audited the entire codebase first for every Portfolio-adjacent term the mission named, and found a genuine, important collision before freezing anything: `lib/academy/portfolio.ts`/`app/teacher/academy/portfolio/page.tsx`/`components/academy/PortfolioView.tsx` already implement a "Portfolio" — but it is **EduNexus Academy's teacher professional-development badge system** (a teacher's completed training modules, missions, reflections, tool-usage counts, and earned badges), not anything learner-facing. Confirmed by reading `PortfolioData`'s full type and `getPortfolioData()`'s implementation — every field is teacher-scoped, zero learner-achievement concept exists anywhere in the module. Documented this explicitly as a **naming-collision risk, not an ownership decision**: the new Learner Portfolio domain must never be implemented under `lib/academy/` and must disambiguate "Learner Portfolio" from "Academy (Teacher) Portfolio" on first mention in any future document. Every other searched term (Projects, Competitions, Certificates, Awards, Achievements, Leadership, Community Service, evidence/file uploads, media library) returned no existing domain, table, or storage infrastructure — confirmed a new canonical domain is the correct, non-duplicative outcome, per the mission's own "never invent a new domain if one already exists" instruction, verified rather than assumed. `docs/architecture/adr-0011-learner-portfolio-architecture.md` freezes: Phase 2 (one permanent definition — "the learner's own curated collection of demonstrated work, achievements, and creations," distinguished from Report Card/Blueprint/Compass/Career/Teacher Reflection/Evidence/Behaviour), Phase 3 (a fifteen-section ownership matrix, one owner each, Portfolio owns only Portfolio), Phase 4 (Blueprint references Portfolio, one-directional, summary-plus-link only, permanent — mirroring Learning Compass/Career's existing Blueprint treatment), Phase 5 (Portfolio ≠ Report Card — score vs. built/achieved, neither references the other's authority), Phase 6 (Career may read Portfolio, Portfolio never predicts), Phase 7 (the most important boundary: Portfolio references Evidence, never copies it — mirroring `learner_projections`' own existing `supporting_evidence_ids` traceability pattern), Phase 8 (the educational philosophy, closing on the user-recommended, verbatim-adopted principle: "Every learner graduates with a portfolio, not just a transcript"), Phase 9 (paper is a small summary only, digital is everything, QR is the bridge — never dozens of printed pages), Phase 10 (all fifteen sections specified across ten dimensions each: purpose/audience/owner/freshness/visibility/QR/max size/snapshot/evidence source/future extensions), Phase 11 (a six-audience visibility matrix governed by one learner-and-school-controlled publish state, not per-section special cases — nothing University/Employer-visible by default), Phase 12 (fifteen future extensions reserved by name only), Phase 13 (nine architectural risks documented, each tied to the specific ADR constraint that prevents it), Phase 14 (zero conflicts confirmed against the Educational Constitution, RAS §3/§10, and ADR-0004 through ADR-0010).

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0005-learner-blueprint-architecture.md` §3, `adr-0006-blueprint-educational-experience.md` (Evidence→Meaning→Action), `adr-0007-blueprint-layout-and-experience.md` §11 (QR philosophy), `adr-0008-blueprint-lifecycle-and-rendering.md` Part 3/8/9 (Snapshot precedent, paper/digital philosophy, traceability), `adr-0009-blueprint-presentation-architecture.md`, `adr-0010-parent-experience-architecture.md` (visibility-matrix pattern this ADR's Phase 11 directly extends), `reference-architecture-specification.md` §3 (Canonical Domain Standards), §10 rules 7–8 (no duplicated business logic, no cross-domain ownership), Educational Constitution Articles I/II/VI/IX/XI.

**ADR**: `docs/architecture/adr-0011-learner-portfolio-architecture.md` — new, required per Guardian mode's own trigger conditions (introduces a new canonical domain).

**Tests added**: none — no code was written this sprint.

**Rollback considerations**: none — this sprint produced only two documents (the ADR and the sprint doc) plus this log entry. Fully reversible by definition; nothing else in the codebase depends on it yet.

## 2026-07-17 — Sprint 12S: Parent Action Centre (Blueprint Guided Actions)

**What changed**: built the Parent Action Centre — "what can the parent do next," the final layer after Blueprint ("who is this learner becoming") and Growth Timeline ("how has this learner grown"). Audited the entire codebase first (Phase 1) and found no canonical parent-action engine exists — instead, **six independent, uncoordinated "tell the parent what to do" generators** already existed: `lib/academicClinic/reportGenerator.ts`'s `buildParentAction`, the **legacy pre-Sprint-12G Blueprint predecessor** `lib/learnerIntelligence/blueprint.ts`'s own `buildParentAction`, `lib/parentPulse/builder.ts`'s `buildParentAction`, `lib/holiday/planner.ts`'s AI-generated per-week `parent_action`, `lib/career/parentIntelligence.ts`'s capability-dimension conversation starters, and Blueprint's own single deterministic `composeParentSummary` action field (the only canonical one of the six). None was reusable as-is (each either reads a domain directly, bypassing Blueprint, or is AI-generated — both forbidden by this sprint's "never recalculates, never predicts" mandate) and none was touched or duplicated — the new Action Centre is a genuinely new, seventh system built exclusively from Blueprint's own already-composed sections. New `lib/parentExperience/actions.ts`: a `ParentAction` model (title/description/actionType/priority/sourceDomain/destination/available/reasonUnavailable/generatedAt) and `composeParentActions()` — pure, synchronous, zero DB access, mapping exactly Phase 2's frozen six-action ownership table (Continue Holiday Learning→Learning Compass, Read Teacher Reflection→Teacher Reflection, Review Attendance→Attendance, Celebrate Achievement→Blueprint Snapshot, View Report Card→Report Cards, Explore Career Journey→Career Intelligence, No Action Needed→Parent Action Centre itself) onto Blueprint's already-composed sibling sections plus one Blueprint Snapshot read. A shared threshold constant (`ATTENDANCE_ATTENTION_THRESHOLD_PERCENT`, extracted from `composeParentSummary.ts`, which already used the literal `90` inline) is now exported and reused by both files — fixing a duplicate-constant risk before it could happen, the same discipline Sprint 12M applied after-the-fact to `HOLIDAY_PLAN_RELEVANCE_DAYS`. Blueprint gained exactly one new section, **Recommended Next Steps** (`recommendedNextSteps`), via new thin composer `lib/learnerBlueprint/composeRecommendedNextSteps.ts` — the first real consumer of Sprint 12L-R's `getLatestBlueprintSnapshot()`, which had been built but never wired to anything. At most one `critical`-priority action is now structurally guaranteed (not just true by accident): a fixed precedence list demotes every candidate beyond the first critical-eligible one to `important` rather than dropping it. New `components/parent/ParentActionCard.tsx` renders "Today's Actions" at the top of Parent Home, reading `blueprint.recommendedNextSteps.data.actions` directly off the same `composeBlueprint()` result the page already fetches — no second call to the composer anywhere in Parent Portal code. The full Blueprint view (both Teacher's `BlueprintView.tsx` and Parent's `ParentBlueprintView.tsx`) also gained a shared `RecommendedNextStepsSection` content renderer, reused verbatim by both audiences. One deliberate, documented layering exception: Blueprint's new composer imports from `lib/parentExperience/actions.ts` (normally downstream of Blueprint per ADR-0010 Part 8) — done because the mission explicitly named that file path and because the same selection logic must be reusable by both Blueprint's own section and any future Parent Experience surface; the constitutional data flow (Evidence → Domain Intelligence → Blueprint → Action Centre, never Evidence → Action Centre directly) is preserved in substance since the function's only inputs are Blueprint's own already-domain-sourced sections.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0008-blueprint-lifecycle-and-rendering.md` Part 5/6 (compose, never calculate), `adr-0009-blueprint-presentation-architecture.md` (one renderer per section, reused across audiences), `adr-0010-parent-experience-architecture.md` Part 3/4/6/7 (visibility matrix extended with a new "Recommended Next Steps: Yes" row, plain language, no internal identifiers, no colour-only meaning), `sprint-12l-blueprint-historical-viewer-refinement.md` (`getLatestBlueprintSnapshot`, wired to a real consumer for the first time this sprint), `sprint-12q-parent-portal-foundation.md`/`sprint-12r-parent-growth-timeline.md` (the existing routes every action's `destination` reuses).

**ADR**: None new — this sprint implements the mission's own frozen ownership table and ADR-0008/0009/0010 exactly as already established; introduces no new canonical domain, ownership, or calculation.

**Tests added**: `lib/parentExperience/actions.pure.test.ts` (new, 13 tests, DB-free): the full Phase 11 checklist (missing Compass/Career/Reflection/Attendance each correctly suppress their action; a report-card snapshot produces View Report Card with the correct destination; any celebration-worthy snapshot produces Celebrate Achievement linking to that exact snapshot; an end-of-term-only snapshot produces neither; an empty learner produces exactly one "No Action Needed" with the exact frozen copy; mixed actions produce every available domain's action with no duplicates; priority ordering never regresses) plus two additional tests directly proving the critical-action cap and every action's field completeness. Full regression re-run: `composeBlueprint.pure.test.ts`/`composeBlueprint.integration.test.ts` (fixtures updated for the new required section), `snapshot.test.ts`, `reflection.integration.test.ts`, `parentPortal.integration.test.ts`, `growthTimeline.pure.test.ts` — 50/50 combined, zero regressions. `tsc --noEmit` clean; `eslint` clean on every touched/new file; `npx next build` completed successfully (no new routes added this sprint, so no collision risk).

**Rollback considerations**: fully reversible, no migration, no schema change. `LearnerBlueprint`'s type gained a new required field (`recommendedNextSteps`) — every existing fixture/consumer was updated in this sprint (`composeBlueprint.pure.test.ts`, `growthTimeline.pure.test.ts`, `validation.ts`'s `ALL_SECTIONS`); any future test file constructing a full `LearnerBlueprint` fixture from scratch will need the same field added. `composeParentSummary.ts`'s inline `90` literal became an exported constant — a behavior-preserving refactor, not a value change.

## 2026-07-17 — Sprint 12R: Parent Growth Timeline & Educational Journey

**What changed**: implemented the Parent Growth Timeline as a pure presentation layer over already-immutable Blueprint Snapshots — zero recomputation, zero new repositories/services, zero schema changes. Re-audited existing historical surfaces first (Phase 1) and found Sprint 12Q's own `/child/[learnerId]/history` page was already fetching each snapshot's full `blueprint_payload` via `listBlueprintSnapshots()` but discarding everything except `snapshot_type`/`created_at` — this sprint's richer cards close that gap using data already on the wire, not a new query, and the page was **enhanced in place** rather than duplicated into a second parallel history route. New `lib/parentExperience/growthTimeline.ts` — two pure functions, no database access: `compareSnapshots(current, previous)` compares four already-rendered fields (attendance percentage, learning-focus subject, teacher-reflection version, career cluster) between one snapshot and the one immediately before it, returning `improving`/`steady`/`declining`/`unknown` per signal — never a new metric, and always the literal frozen copy "Not enough historical information." whenever either side of a comparison is missing; `buildMilestones(snapshots)` labels each already-fetched row by its existing `snapshot_type`. A deliberate, documented omission: generic "Promotion" is not a milestone type, since ADR-0008 Part 3 only ever froze three Snapshot triggers (report card publication, end of term, graduation) — a routine promotion has no snapshot to point a milestone at, and reading `learner_promotions` directly to fabricate one would violate this sprint's own "Blueprint Snapshots only" scope. New page `/child/[learnerId]/journey` (Growth Journey — the milestone-level landing page Phase 6's navigation flow required between Parent Home and the Timeline) links into the enhanced Timeline, which links into the unmodified Sprint 12Q Snapshot detail page. Parent Home's own journey teaser card now points at `/journey` instead of directly at `/history`, matching the frozen four-step hierarchy (Parent Home → Growth Journey → Timeline → Snapshot).

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0008-blueprint-lifecycle-and-rendering.md` Part 3/Part 6 (Snapshot immutability, no recomputation), `adr-0009-blueprint-presentation-architecture.md` (navigation model), `adr-0010-parent-experience-architecture.md` Part 4/5/6 (terminology, action-orientation, information boundaries — all reused verbatim from Sprint 12Q's `terminology.ts`), `sprint-12q-parent-portal-foundation.md` (the Timeline/Snapshot pages this sprint extends rather than duplicates).

**ADR**: None new — this sprint implements ADR-0008/0009/0010 exactly as already frozen; introduces no new canonical domain, ownership, or calculation.

**Tests added**: `lib/parentExperience/growthTimeline.pure.test.ts` (new, 5 tests, DB-free): no-previous-snapshot returns "unknown" for every signal; attendance improving/declining/steady detected correctly from real percentages; a comparison is never fabricated when either side is unavailable; a teacher-reflection version change is detected as "improving" without ever diffing the reflection's actual text; milestones mark the earliest snapshot correctly and never include a fabricated "Promotion" entry. All 5 passing. Full regression re-run: `composeBlueprint.pure.test.ts`/`composeBlueprint.integration.test.ts`, `snapshot.test.ts`, `reflection.integration.test.ts`, `parentPortal.integration.test.ts` — 37/37 combined, zero regressions. `tsc --noEmit` clean; `eslint` clean on every touched/new file; `npx next build` succeeds with `/child/[learnerId]/journey` in the route manifest, no collisions.

**Rollback considerations**: fully reversible, no migration, no schema change. Two pre-existing Sprint 12Q files were modified (not just added-to): `/child/[learnerId]/history/page.tsx` (richer cards, same data source) and `/child/[learnerId]/page.tsx` (one link retargeted from `/history` to `/journey`) — both are presentation-only changes, no data-path or permission-check behavior changed in either file.

## 2026-07-17 — Sprint 12Q: Parent Portal Foundation (Implementation)

**What changed**: implemented ADR-0010's Parent Experience architecture as the first real Parent Portal surface — a presentation layer only, zero new educational calculations. Re-audited existing parent-facing routes for reuse before writing anything (Phase 1) and found a concrete routing collision `next build` caught immediately: the mission's natural `/blueprint` path was already owned by `app/(student)/blueprint/page.tsx` (the learner's own self-view, a distinct pre-existing feature outside this sprint's original audit scope) — resolved by using `/child` as the Parent Portal's path segment instead, confirmed by a clean production build listing all five new routes. Built five pages under `app/(parent)/child/`: `/child` (entry point — auto-redirects a single-child parent straight to their Home, shows a plain list for multiple children, reusing `resolveParent()`/`repos.learners` only), `/child/[learnerId]` (Parent Home — one `composeBlueprint()` call feeds every teaser card: Identity, Parent Summary headline, Teacher Reflection preview, Attendance percentage, Compass focus, Career cluster, plus one `getLatestBlueprintSnapshot()` call for the journey teaser), `/child/[learnerId]/full` (the full Blueprint, Parent-filtered), `/child/[learnerId]/history` (calls `listBlueprintSnapshots()` only), and `/child/[learnerId]/history/[snapshotId]` (calls `getBlueprintSnapshot()` only) — the identical four Blueprint/Snapshot functions Teacher Blueprint already calls, no new repository or service anywhere. New `components/parent/ParentSectionCard.tsx` replaces `BlueprintSectionCard` as the Parent-facing chrome specifically to satisfy ADR-0010 Part 6 ("Owner strings — Never"): it never renders `section.owner` and uses new `lib/parentExperience/terminology.ts` (a pure rename lookup, ADR-0010 Part 4's frozen table) instead of raw status/freshness enum values. New `components/parent/ParentBlueprintView.tsx` renders exactly the seven Blueprint sections ADR-0010 Part 3 marks Yes/Summary-only (Identity, Academic Record, Attendance, Learning Compass, Career, Teacher Reflection, Parent Summary), omitting the three marked No/Future (Evidence Trail, Educational Identity, Growth Timeline) entirely — but every section's actual *content* renderer (`IdentitySection`, `AttendanceSection`, etc.) is reused verbatim, unchanged, from `components/blueprint/sections.tsx`, so there is exactly one place educational content renders, never a second copy. `HistoricalBanner` is likewise reused verbatim for the Current/Historical distinction. A documented gap, not silently worked around: no learner photo is shown, even though `learners.photo_url` exists at the database layer, because Blueprint's own `IdentityData` doesn't carry it and reaching around `composeBlueprint()` to query it directly would violate Phase 3's "consume Blueprint directly, no second read path" rule — closing this is a future Blueprint-domain change, out of this presentation-only sprint's scope. Five `loading.tsx` files added, all reusing the existing shared skeleton components from Sprint 12L-R (zero new skeletons). Every route uses `requireAuthentication` + the pre-existing `requireParent` — no new permission system.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0010-parent-experience-architecture.md` (every Part — this is its first implementation), `adr-0008-blueprint-lifecycle-and-rendering.md` Part 5/6 (Blueprint composes, never recalculates — verified zero calculation code in any new file), `adr-0009-blueprint-presentation-architecture.md` (the presentation-layer discipline this sprint's reuse strategy follows), `sprint-12k-blueprint-snapshots.md`/`sprint-12l-blueprint-historical-viewer.md`/`sprint-12l-blueprint-historical-viewer-refinement.md` (the Snapshot/History/loading/empty-state patterns reused directly).

**ADR**: None new — this sprint implements ADR-0010 exactly as frozen; introduces no new canonical domain, ownership, or calculation.

**Tests added**: `lib/parentExperience/parentPortal.integration.test.ts` (new, 3 tests against real synthetic data): a real linked guardian passes `requireParent` and can compose the identical Blueprint any Parent Portal page renders, plus read snapshot history/latest-snapshot correctly; an unlinked stranger is rejected; a guardian of one learner cannot access a different, unrelated learner's data even across schools. All 3 passing. Full regression re-run: `composeBlueprint.pure.test.ts`/`composeBlueprint.integration.test.ts`, `snapshot.test.ts`, `reflection.integration.test.ts` — 29/29 combined, zero regressions. `tsc --noEmit` clean project-wide; `eslint` clean on every touched/new file; `npx next build` succeeds with all five new routes in the production route manifest.

**Rollback considerations**: fully reversible, no migration, no schema change — every change is new, additive files (pages, components, one terminology module) under `app/(parent)/child/`, `components/parent/`, and `lib/parentExperience/`. No existing file was modified except none — this sprint touched zero pre-existing files, only added new ones, so there is nothing to roll back in the existing codebase even in principle.

## 2026-07-17 — Sprint 12P: Parent Experience Architecture

**What changed**: architecture and documentation only, per explicit mission instruction — no code, page, route, API, database change, notification, or message was written. Audited every existing parent-facing feature in the codebase (Phase 1) before freezing anything, and found a real, previously undocumented problem: **at least five independently-built systems currently generate parent-facing "what should the parent understand" content**, each with its own tone, data source, and vocabulary, none reading from or agreeing with each other — Blueprint's own deterministic `composeParentSummary`; `lib/parentPulse/builder.ts`'s AI-adjacent weekly WhatsApp digest (sourced from Projection + legacy `learnerModel` fields); `lib/career/parentIntelligence.ts`'s capability-dimension conversation starters (a completely separate vocabulary from Blueprint's own Career section); `lib/holiday/planner.ts`'s genuinely AI-generated (DeepSeek) parent WhatsApp message; and `lib/academicClinic/reportGenerator.ts`'s own `buildParentAction`. Also found three near-identically-named parent career routes (`app/(parent)/career-intelligence`, `career-intelligence-report`, `career-report`) not yet reconciled with each other or with Sprint 12N's Blueprint restriction to cluster-level-only career content. None of this was fixed this sprint (architecture-only scope) — instead, `docs/architecture/adr-0010-parent-experience-architecture.md` freezes the definition, journey, visibility matrix, terminology table, and — critically — a permanent one-directional rule (Blueprint feeds Parent Experience, never the reverse) that gives the eventual reconciliation of these five systems an unambiguous target to converge on, the same "document precisely now, fix in its own scoped sprint later" discipline Sprint 12N already applied to a different duplicate-pipeline finding. ADR-0010 freezes: Part 1 (one-sentence definition, explicitly not monitoring/surveillance/pressure), Part 2 (ten-step Parent Journey, Welcome through School Communication, with documented rationale per step), Part 3 (visibility matrix for every current/near-future Blueprint section — Yes/Summary only/Partial/Never/Future), Part 4 (a twelve-row terminology table, e.g. Capability Projection → Learning Strengths, Risk Index → Needs Extra Support — the first shared vocabulary layer across the five systems Phase 1 found), Part 5 (every screen must answer "what can I do"), Part 6 (an explicit never-show list — internal AI confidence, teacher drafting notes, evidence debugging, identity-bridge state, repository/version identifiers), Part 7 (an emotional-design test: informed/encouraged/included/hopeful, never judged/overwhelmed/scared/confused/compared, and never any cross-learner ranking or comparison), Part 8 (Blueprint → Parent Experience, one direction, permanently), Part 9 (Report Card / Blueprint / Parent Experience as three non-competing purposes), Part 10 (ten future extensions reserved by name only, not designed).

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0005-learner-blueprint-architecture.md`, `adr-0006-blueprint-educational-experience.md`, `adr-0007-blueprint-layout-and-experience.md` §14, `adr-0008-blueprint-lifecycle-and-rendering.md` Parts 3/4/5/9, `adr-0009-blueprint-presentation-architecture.md` (5-layer presentation hierarchy, the real `app/(parent)/*` surfaces it already catalogued), `sprint-12o-teacher-reflection-domain.md` (Teacher Reflection's placement in the new Parent Journey), Educational Constitution Articles I/II/VI/IX/XI.

**ADR**: `docs/architecture/adr-0010-parent-experience-architecture.md` — new, required per Guardian mode's own trigger conditions (introduces a new architectural layer, even though not a new canonical domain).

**Tests added**: none — no code was written this sprint.

**Rollback considerations**: none — this sprint produced only two documents (the ADR and the sprint doc) plus this log entry. Fully reversible by definition; nothing else in the codebase depends on it yet.

## 2026-07-17 — Sprint 12O: Teacher Reflection Domain & Blueprint Integration

**What changed**: audited the entire codebase for every existing teacher-comment/narrative field (Phase 1) before writing any schema — found `school_report_cards.class_teacher_comment` (Report-Cards-owned, term-scoped, already correctly documented as reference-only per ADR-0005 §5), assessment-level `teacher_comments`, and a holiday-return comment field, none of which are duplicates; and one genuinely adjacent system, `lib/remarks/evidence.ts` ("teacher remarks as Evidence"), which stores free-text teacher observations as raw, confidence-scored input to the Projection Engine — a fundamentally different purpose (evidence for intelligence, never a finished published narrative) from what this sprint builds, deliberately left untouched. Confirmed no existing table fits, and built the first genuinely new canonical domain since Blueprint Snapshots: `teacher_reflections` (migration `20260717150000_teacher_reflections.sql`, applied to the real project after explicit user confirmation) — Core-native (`learner_id`/`school_id`, matching Blueprint Snapshots' own convention), versioned (`UNIQUE(learner_id, version)`, one row per reflection cycle, never edited across cycles), `draft`/`published` lifecycle enforced at three layers matching `learner_evidence`/`blueprint_snapshots`' own precedent (service-level pre-checks for clean errors, no repository method that could plausibly touch a published row's content, and a database trigger — `enforce_teacher_reflection_immutability` — that unconditionally rejects UPDATE/DELETE on any row where `status = 'published'`, verified directly against the real database for both operations). New `TeacherReflectionRepository` (`createReflection`/`updateReflection`/`publishReflection`/`findCurrent`/`history`, no business logic) and new service `lib/teacherReflection/reflection.ts` (`createDraft`/`updateDraft`/`publish`/`findCurrent`/`history` — owns permission checks via the existing `requireSchoolStaff` gate, versioning arithmetic, publish-time signature capture via the existing `TeacherRepository.findProfileFullName`, and validation). Blueprint integration: `composeTeacherReflection()` now calls `findCurrent()` exactly once — its `status` is `'available'`/`'unavailable'`, never `'not_implemented'` again (that status correctly meant "no domain exists yet," which stopped being true this sprint); a draft-in-progress never leaks into Blueprint (proven directly: Blueprint stays `unavailable` while a draft exists, becomes `available` only after `publish()`). New `TeacherReflectionSection` renderer (`components/blueprint/sections.tsx`) wired into `BlueprintView.tsx`, replacing the placeholder `{() => null}`. `TeacherReflectionData`'s type was corrected from ADR-0006 §6's speculative field list (`reflection`/`parentPartnership`, never actually populated) to match what the real domain stores (`strengths`/`growthArea`/`learningHabits`/`recommendedSupport`/`holidayFocus`/`teacherSignature`/`writtenAt`/`publishedAt`/`version`). Two deliberate scope decisions documented, not silently made: (1) the ownership gate for writing is `requireSchoolStaff` (any active school staff), not a true "assigned class teacher only" check — building that would require bridging Core's class/learner relationship into the legacy-scoped `requireClassTeacher`, a real future refinement outside this sprint's explicit ask; (2) no teacher-facing authoring UI/route was built — the mission's own phases and Deliverables list never asked for one, so the full lifecycle is proven directly against the service layer instead.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0005-learner-blueprint-architecture.md` §2.7, `docs/architecture/adr-0006-blueprint-educational-experience.md` §6 (Teacher Reflection's originally-reserved slot, now filled), `docs/architecture/adr-0008-blueprint-lifecycle-and-rendering.md` Part 5/6 (Blueprint asks, never computes), RAS §10.7/§10.8 (single-owner encapsulation), CLAUDE.md (teacher_id as attribution never access-gate; DB-trigger-enforced immutability).

**ADR**: None — a new canonical domain was created, but per Guardian mode's own ADR trigger conditions this doesn't require one: it doesn't touch an *existing* canonical identity, doesn't change ownership of anything that already existed, and doesn't conflict with the RAS — it fills a reserved, already-anticipated slot (ADR-0006 §6) exactly as designed, the same non-trigger reasoning Sprint 12K applied to Blueprint Snapshots.

**Tests added**: `lib/teacherReflection/reflection.integration.test.ts` (new, 3 tests: full lifecycle including DB-level immutability proof for both UPDATE and DELETE on a published row; second-cycle versioning proving a new cycle is a new row, never an edit; required-field validation). `composeBlueprint.integration.test.ts`: one new test proving the full Blueprint-visible lifecycle (unavailable before any reflection, still unavailable during an unpublished draft, available with real captured content — including `teacherSignature` — only after publish), plus one corrected assertion (`teacherReflection.status` is now `'unavailable'`, not the old `'not_implemented'`, for a learner with no reflection yet). Combined regression across all suites touched this sprint: 48/48 passing, zero regressions in Identity, Attendance, Career, Compass, Snapshots, or Report Cards. `tsc --noEmit` and `eslint` both clean on every touched/new file.

**Rollback considerations**: the migration is the hard-to-reverse part — `teacher_reflections` now exists on the real project with live triggers (same discipline as every prior schema-adding sprint this series). All code changes are reversible. `composeTeacherReflection()`'s signature changed from zero arguments to `(coreLearnerId, schoolId)`, and its return type changed from sync to async — the one call site (`composeBlueprint.ts`) was updated in this sprint. `TeacherReflectionData`'s shape changed entirely (old speculative fields removed) — the two consumers (`composeTeacherReflection.ts`, the new `TeacherReflectionSection`) were both written fresh in this sprint, so nothing else could have depended on the old shape (it was never populated with real data before now).

## 2026-07-17 — Sprint 12N: Career Intelligence ↔ Learner Blueprint Integration

**What changed**: audited the entire Career Intelligence domain (Phase 1) before touching Blueprint and found a real, previously-unnoticed architectural problem: `lib/learnerBlueprint/composeCareer.ts` read career matches via `careerEngine.getMatchesForStudent()`, which pulls from the persisted `career_matches` table — a table written only by `matchEngine.ts`'s AI-generated (DeepSeek) `generateCareerMatches()`, a pipeline entirely separate from the evidence-first, deterministic `computeCapabilityMatches()` pipeline (`lib/career/capabilityMatchEngine.ts`) that the live student Career Explorer, Parent Career Intelligence, and Holiday Planner all actually use via the single canonical `buildCareerIntelligence()` (`lib/learnerIntelligence/careerIntelligence.ts`). Two real consequences: Blueprint's career data could silently disagree with what a student sees on their own Career Explorer for identical evidence (the persisted table isn't kept in sync with Projection the way `buildCareerIntelligence()` is), and — more seriously — Blueprint had no grade gate at all, unconditionally showing a specific predicted career title to Junior (Grade 7–9) learners in direct violation of the platform's Career Principle (broad families only at Junior, never an individual prediction) that `buildCareerIntelligence()` already correctly enforces elsewhere. Fixed by switching Blueprint onto the already-correct canonical function: new `getCareerBlueprintSummary(studentId)` in Career's own owning module (`lib/learnerIntelligence/careerIntelligence.ts`) calls `buildCareerIntelligence()` exactly once and selects only the top pre-sorted family/match, returning a narrowed, Blueprint-safe shape (`careerCluster`, `strengthProfile`, `futureDirection`, `aiOutlook`, `confidence`, `version`) that deliberately excludes any specific career/job title — matching this sprint's explicit Architectural Goal ("orientation, never job selection"). One minimal, additive change inside Career Intelligence itself: `CareerMatchInsight` (Senior mode) gained a `careerCategory` field, propagating a value `computeCapabilityMatches()` already computes but previously dropped, so the new summary function doesn't need a second call into the matching engine to learn a match's cluster. `composeCareer.ts` rewritten to call only the new function; `CareerData` (Blueprint's own type) narrowed to match — `careerTitle` removed entirely (compile-time enforced, not just convention), `strengthSnapshot`/`futureReadiness` renamed to `strengthProfile`/`futureDirection`, and the old always-null `futureReadiness` gap resolved into a real `confidence` field (Career Intelligence already has a canonical confidence-label export, `confidenceFromAssessmentCount()` — unlike Learning Compass's still-open readiness gap from Sprint 12M). `CareerSection` (`components/blueprint/sections.tsx`) updated to render exactly the mission's Phase 9 field list. Two gaps confirmed and left honestly null, not invented: AI Outlook (no canonical cluster-level market-outlook function exists — `kenya_market_outlook` is per-specific-career metadata, and Blueprint no longer surfaces a specific career to attach it to) and algorithm Version (no version export exists for the capability match engine). A real, previously-hidden bug was also fixed as a side effect of correct wiring: a bridged learner with zero evidence used to get Career section `status: 'available'` with every field silently `null` (misrepresenting that data existed) — now correctly `status: 'unavailable'` with the mission's exact specified reason string.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0006-blueprint-educational-experience.md` §4 (the five frozen Career fields, the "select never re-derive" corollary), `docs/architecture/adr-0005-learner-blueprint-architecture.md` §2.5 (Career ownership), RAS §10.7/§10.8 (single-owner encapsulation, tightened further by removing the deprecated read path entirely from Blueprint's reachable code).

**ADR**: None — no new canonical domain/identity/ownership; the deprecated `career_matches`/AI-matching pathway itself was not touched, removed, or redesigned (still used by other, unrelated legacy consumers) — only Blueprint's own read was switched to the already-correct canonical function, a routing fix, not an architecture change.

**Tests added**: `lib/learnerBlueprint/composeBlueprint.integration.test.ts` — one new test creating a real bridged learner with a real recorded assessment (via `createBridgedAssessment`/`recordBridgedMarks`, the same production path `academicBridge.test.ts`'s own end-to-end test uses) and asserting Career becomes `available` with a real cluster label, real `confidence`, real `futureDirection`, and no `careerTitle` field anywhere on the object. The existing zero-evidence bridged test's assertions were corrected to the new, intentional Unavailable-with-reason behaviour. Full suite: `composeBlueprint.pure.test.ts`/`composeBlueprint.integration.test.ts`/`snapshot.test.ts` (26/26) + `reportCardOwnership.security.test.ts`/`reportCardPublicationGuard.integration.test.ts`/`endOfTermFullChain.test.ts`/`granularEndOfTermFlow.test.ts` (19/19) — 45/45 combined, zero regressions. `tsc --noEmit` and `eslint` both clean on every touched/new file.

**Rollback considerations**: fully reversible, no migration, no schema change. `CareerData`'s shape change is a breaking type change for any Blueprint consumer expecting `careerTitle`/`strengthSnapshot`/`futureReadiness` — the only two consumers (`composeCareer.ts`, `CareerSection`) were both updated in this sprint. Historical Blueprint Snapshots taken before this sprint keep their old `CareerData` shape frozen inside `blueprint_payload` (by design, per Sprint 12K's schema-versioning discipline) — `CareerSection` reads the new field names defensively, so an old snapshot's career section renders with its (now-absent) fields simply not shown, rather than crashing.

## 2026-07-17 — Sprint 12M: Learning Compass ↔ Learner Blueprint Integration

**What changed**: audited every Learning Compass function Blueprint's Learning Compass section could read from (Phase 1) and confirmed the existing composer already duplicated no business logic — but found a real bug: `lib/learnerBlueprint/composeLearningCompass.ts` (Sprint 12G) had its own local `HOLIDAY_PLAN_RELEVANCE_DAYS = 90`, silently diverging from the canonical constant already in `lib/holiday/types.ts` (`= 45`, explicitly documented there as shared so Blueprint and the student Holiday page "agree on when a plan has gone stale") — a duplicate constant with a wrong value, in violation of CLAUDE.md's "no duplicate constant definitions across files." Fixed by consolidating Blueprint's two separate Compass reads (`getNextSubject()` directly, `repos.learnerIntelligence.findPublishedHolidayPlan()` directly) into one new canonical function, `lib/compass/summary.ts`'s `getLearningCompassSummary(studentId)`, which internally imports the shared constant instead of redefining it. `composeLearningCompass()` now calls exactly this one function. Two scope items from the mission brief were deliberately **not** implemented and are documented as gaps rather than worked around: (1) Holiday Programme Duration/Start Date — ADR-0006 §3 only ever froze a single "Holiday Programme availability" boolean for Blueprint, and `HolidayPlanData` has no `start_date` field to read (only a free-text `holiday_period`), so adding these would mean either fabricating data or exceeding the ADR without an amendment; (2) the Learning Compass QR — audited the entire codebase and confirmed no QR generation/rendering system exists anywhere yet (ADR-0007 §11 only design-froze nine future destinations), and this sprint's own Forbidden list explicitly forbids building a QR generator, so nothing was added. Learning Readiness remains `null` (pre-existing, re-confirmed gap: Compass has no readiness-label function and no raw-score function either).

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0006-blueprint-educational-experience.md` §3 (the five frozen Learning Compass fields, and the "summarize never re-derive" corollary), `docs/architecture/adr-0007-blueprint-layout-and-experience.md` §5/§11 (Learning Compass snapshot positioning, QR destinations as design-only), RAS §10.7/§10.8 (single-owner encapsulation — tightened by this sprint from "two direct reads" to "one canonical function").

**ADR**: None — no new canonical domain/identity/ownership; the two undelivered items (Holiday sub-fields, QR) require either an ADR-0006 amendment or a future QR-implementation sprint, neither begun here per the mission's own Forbidden list.

**Tests added**: none new (no new business logic to cover); full regression re-run instead — `composeBlueprint.pure.test.ts`/`composeBlueprint.integration.test.ts` (includes two tests asserting `learningCompass.status` directly) and `snapshot.test.ts`, 25/25 combined, zero regressions. `tsc --noEmit` and `eslint` both clean on `lib/compass/summary.ts` (new) and `lib/learnerBlueprint/composeLearningCompass.ts` (edited).

**Rollback considerations**: fully reversible, no migration, no schema change. The `HOLIDAY_PLAN_RELEVANCE_DAYS` fix changes Blueprint's holiday-relevance window from 90 days to 45 days (now matching the rest of the platform) — a behavior change, but a bug fix aligning to the already-canonical value other consumers use, not a new business rule.

## 2026-07-17 — Sprint 12L-R: Blueprint Historical Viewer Refinement

**What changed**: closed the genuine gaps between Sprint 12K's already-shipped Blueprint Snapshots/Historical Viewer and a fresh "Sprint 12L" mission brief that, on audit, turned out to already be fully implemented — re-building it would have created a duplicate read path in violation of Phase B's engineering rules. Verified all nine Sprint 12K claims directly against current code first (Phase 1), then implemented only the real deltas: (1) `BlueprintSnapshotRepository.getLatestForLearner()` + `lib/learnerBlueprint/snapshot.ts`'s `getLatestBlueprintSnapshot()` — a pure read using the same ordering `listForLearner` already had, not wired to any consumer yet since none exists; (2) `loading.tsx` for all three Blueprint routes (Current, History list, Snapshot detail), backed by two new shared skeletons in `components/ui/skeletons.tsx` (`BlueprintSkeleton`, `BlueprintHistoryListSkeleton`), matching this codebase's existing `app/dashboard/*/loading.tsx` pattern; (3) a new shared `components/blueprint/BlueprintStateMessage.tsx` replacing the previous blanket `notFound()` for every failure with four explicit states (learner-not-found, snapshot-not-found, permission-denied, unavailable) — `MembershipRequiredError` deliberately still resolves to `notFound()` (existence-hiding preserved for non-members), while `PermissionDeniedError` (a confirmed member lacking the staff role) now gets an honest explicit message, since there's nothing left to hide from a known member; (4) `HistoricalBanner` gained School/Schema Version/Blueprint Version/Generated From, every one read from an existing row column or payload field, no recomputation, no new schema; (5) accessibility additions only (`aria-expanded`, `aria-label` landmarks, `role="alert"`/`role="status"`, focus-visible rings, "Most Recent"/"Current" text badges alongside existing colour cues) and timeline-clarity wording, no redesign.

## 2026-07-17 — Sprint 12K: Blueprint Snapshots & Historical Viewer

**What changed**: implemented immutable Blueprint Snapshots and the Historical Viewer, per ADR-0008 Part 3 and this sprint's own explicit instruction to audit before adding storage. **Audit first**: checked every existing table shaped like a reusable snapshot/report mechanism (`school_intelligence_snapshots` — wrong domain, a mutable weekly Analytics rollup, not learner-scoped; `academic_reports`/`student_clinic_reports`/`clinic_reports` — Academic-Clinic-owned, legacy `student_id`-keyed, forbidden domain; `school_report_cards` — deliberately a *different* artifact per ADR-0008 Part 3's own three-way distinction) and confirmed none fit — `blueprint_snapshots` is genuinely new, not a second learner-record store. Applied migration `20260717120000_blueprint_snapshots.sql` to the real project (confirmed with the user first, matching the Sprint 12B precedent): `learner_id`/`school_id`/`academic_year_id`/`term_id`/`snapshot_type` (CHECK'd to exactly the three ADR-0008 Part 3 triggers)/`blueprint_payload` (jsonb, exactly `composeBlueprint()`'s output, no derived analytics)/`provenance`/`schema_version`/`created_at`/`created_by`, RLS (school-staff read-only, no write policy for `authenticated` at all), and — matching `learner_evidence`'s own precedent — a database trigger that unconditionally rejects UPDATE/DELETE even from the service-role client, verified directly against the real database. New `BlueprintSnapshotRepository` (insert/findById/listForLearner only — no update/delete method exists, matching the trigger). New `lib/learnerBlueprint/snapshot.ts` service (`createBlueprintSnapshot`/`getBlueprintSnapshot`/`listBlueprintSnapshots`) — composes via the same canonical `composeBlueprint()` every other consumer uses, then freezes that exact result; no second computation anywhere. Wired all three frozen triggers, non-fatally: `lib/core/report-cards.ts`'s `publishReportCards()` (now snapshot-type-parameterized so it produces `report_card_publication` when called standalone and `end_of_term` when called from `lib/core/endOfTerm.ts`'s `runEndOfTerm` — one code path, correctly typed by real calling context, never two near-duplicate snapshots for one real event), and `lib/core/promotions.ts`'s `runAnnualPromotion` (graduation). Deliberately **awaited, not fire-and-forget** — an un-awaited promise can be killed mid-flight when a serverless function's response returns before it resolves; failures are caught and logged, never thrown, so a snapshot failure still can't block or roll back the real operation. Extended `components/blueprint/BlueprintView.tsx` (Sprint 12J) with one new optional `historicalMeta` prop and a new `HistoricalBanner` component — the exact same renderer serves both the Current Blueprint and every Historical Snapshot, zero duplicated rendering logic, per mission. New pages: `/student/blueprint/[learnerId]/history` (snapshot list) and `/student/blueprint/[learnerId]/history/[snapshotId]` (selected snapshot, reusing `BlueprintView`), with one-directional navigation only (Current → History → Selected, "navigate back" permitted, no new cross-links to Compass/Career/Report Cards). **A real bug found and fixed by the new tests, not discovered later**: `learner_promotions.processed_by` already FKs to `school_users(id)`, but `createBlueprintSnapshot()`'s `actorUserId` needs a real `auth.uid()` for its internal permission checks (`composeAttendance`'s admin check) — passing `processedBy` straight through would have silently made every graduation-triggered snapshot's Attendance section wrongly show "Unavailable." Fixed by adding `SchoolRepository.findSchoolUserById()` (the missing reverse lookup) and resolving the real actor id once, non-fatally, before the promotion loop.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0008-blueprint-lifecycle-and-rendering.md` Part 3 (the three frozen triggers, the three-way Report Card/Snapshot/Current-Blueprint distinction), `docs/architecture/adr-0009-blueprint-presentation-architecture.md` §6/§9 (snapshot navigation, Report Card independence), `docs/architecture/sprint-12j-blueprint-ui-phase1.md` (the renderer being reused), `docs/architecture/adr-0004-attendance-integration-principles.md` (the immutability-trigger precedent, mirrored from `learner_evidence`).

**ADR**: None new — implements ADR-0008 Part 3 exactly as already frozen; introduces no new canonical identity, ownership, or lifecycle rule.

**Tests added**: `lib/learnerBlueprint/snapshot.test.ts` (4 tests against real synthetic data: snapshot creation stores the real composed payload; immutability enforced at the database level for both UPDATE and DELETE, even via the service-role client, row confirmed unchanged afterward; the report-card-publication trigger fires correctly end-to-end; the graduation trigger fires correctly end-to-end, after fixing the actor-id bug above). All 4 pass. Full regression re-run: `composeBlueprint.pure.test.ts`/`composeBlueprint.integration.test.ts` (20/20, Sprint 12G/12H unchanged), `reportCardOwnership.security.test.ts`, `reportCardPublicationGuard.integration.test.ts`, `endOfTermFullChain.test.ts`, `granularEndOfTermFlow.test.ts`, `generateReportCards.ranking.test.ts`, `reportCardWithSubjects.test.ts`, `attendanceReportCardIntegration.test.ts` (33/33) — zero regressions from the `publishReportCards`/`promotions.ts` signature changes. `tsc --noEmit` and `eslint` both clean project-wide.

**Rollback considerations**: the migration is the hard-to-reverse part — `blueprint_snapshots` now exists on the real project with live triggers; rolling it back would need its own explicit decision, same discipline as Sprint 12B's Attendance migration. All code changes are reversible. `publishReportCards()` gained a new trailing optional parameter (`snapshotType`, defaulted) and a leading required `actorUserId` parameter — a breaking signature change with three call sites, all updated in this sprint (`app/api/core/reports/route.ts`, `lib/core/endOfTerm.ts`, `lib/core/granularEndOfTermFlow.test.ts`); the worktree-based agent copy at `.claude/worktrees/agent-aedf323a0b5ed2eb3/` still has its own separate, unmodified copy of this file and was deliberately not touched (same "not mine to fix" boundary maintained since Sprint 12C).

Completed the four required architecture corrections from Sprint 12J-A first: added `freshness: 'live'|'snapshot'|'historical'` to `BlueprintSection<T>` (`lib/learnerBlueprint/types.ts`), populated per ADR-0007 §6's table in all nine composers; froze one canonical 19-item reserved-future-module list (`sprint-12e-blueprint-layout-design.md` §17), updating the two other differently-counted "fourteen"/"thirteen other" references in ADR-0008 and ADR-0009's companions to cite it instead of restating their own lists; standardized "Evidence Trail" everywhere in ADR-0009 (previously "Evidence Trace"/"Evidence Timeline" in three places); added an explicit clarifying sentence to ADR-0009 §6 stating the Report Card → Current Blueprint → Historical Snapshots → Compass/Career navigation phrase is an entity ordering, never a literal forward chain (Report Card is a dead-end; Compass/Career are parallel exits). Then built the UI itself: `app/student/blueprint/[learnerId]/page.tsx` (thin Server Component — `requireAuthentication` → `repos.learners.findSchoolId` → `requireSchoolStaff` teacher-first gate → one `composeBlueprint()` call), `components/blueprint/BlueprintView.tsx` (renders the frozen 9-section + Evidence Trail order, audience-neutral, single scrolling document per ADR-0009 §4 — no tabs), `components/blueprint/BlueprintSectionCard.tsx` (the one generic `'use client'` wrapper showing Owner/Freshness/Availability/Last-Updated/expand-collapse for every section, with fixed `Unavailable.`/`Coming Soon.` states per the mission's exact instruction), `components/blueprint/sections.tsx` (six presentation-only renderers for the sections that can be `available` this sprint), and `components/blueprint/EvidenceTrailPlaceholder.tsx` (static, inert, no QR/link, per mission). A full authenticated-browser click-through was attempted (Playwright against the live dev server) but the sandbox blocks the headless browser's own outbound fetch to Supabase's auth endpoint (reproduced twice) — Node-side fetch is unaffected, confirmed by the same script's setup phase succeeding — so verification instead used real synthetic data (school, teacher-onboarded admin, Core learner, one attendance record) composed via the real engine and rendered via `renderToStaticMarkup` against the actual `BlueprintView` component tree: confirmed correct real-data rendering (Identity/Attendance/Parent Summary available with real content, Academic Record/Compass/Career correctly unavailable with real reasons, three placeholders correctly "Coming Soon," zero render exceptions). **Process note kept for the record**: mid-verification, `npm install --no-save playwright` triggered an unintended large dependency re-resolution and briefly crashed the dev server — `package.json`/`package-lock.json` confirmed untouched throughout, `tsc`/`eslint` re-confirmed clean immediately after, Playwright removed from `node_modules` once that verification path was abandoned.
