**What changed**: implemented ADR-0014 in full — the canonical Learner Competitions domain now exists. Full findings and phase-by-phase record in `docs/architecture/sprint-13b-learner-competitions-foundation.md`.

**Re-audit (Phase 1, mandatory, done first)**: re-swept the repository and re-read Achievement/Portfolio/Projects/Blueprint before writing any code. Confirmed no Competition repository, service, schema, or route existed; nothing shipped since Sprint 13A changed the plan.

**What was built**:
- `supabase/migrations/20260719090000_learner_competitions.sql` — additive only, applied to the live project after explicit user approval. Four tables: `learner_competitions` (the one main entity — "Competition" and "Competition Entry" are the same row for v1, per ADR-0014), `competition_members`, `competition_media` (including certificates), `competition_history` (append-only audit trail). Twelve statuses matching ADR-0014 Phase 4 exactly: nine main-line states (Opportunity→Registration→Preparation→Participation→Judging→Results→Verification→Published→Historical) plus three named terminal branches (Rejected from Verification, Withdrawn from Registration/Preparation/Participation, Revoked from Published). Deliberately no separate mentor/results/feedback tables — each is a single fact per Entry, implemented as a column, matching the same table-vs-column judgment every sibling domain in this series makes.
- `lib/repositories/competition.repository.ts` — `CompetitionRepository`, registered as `repos.competitions`. One named method per lifecycle transition; no generic `update()`/`delete()`/`mutate()` — proven by a reflection-based test.
- `lib/learnerCompetitions/{types,validation,competition}.ts` — the canonical service. Owns lifecycle only; zero imports of Blueprint/Portfolio/Achievement/Parent Experience/Career (grep-verified). `recordResults()` implements the Judging→Results→Verification transition as two real, auditable status changes within one service call, since ADR-0014 names Results→Verification as a genuine automatic transition, not a simplification to skip.
- `lib/learnerBlueprint/composeCompetitions.ts`, wired into `composeBlueprint.ts`/`types.ts`/`validation.ts` via the identical 5-file pattern Sprint 12AA's audit documented for every prior domain addition. Returns exactly five fields (`totalCompetitions`, `verifiedCompetitions`, `latestCompetition`, `currentParticipation`, `competitionsUrl`) — judging/feedback are structurally absent from the type, not filtered at runtime; `currentParticipation` exposes only `{name, level, category}` for an in-flight entry, matching Projects' own `currentActiveProject` precedent.
- Immutability enforced at all three required layers (Service/Repository/DB trigger) — `enforce_competition_immutability()` mirrors `enforce_achievement_immutability()`/`enforce_learner_project_immutability()` exactly; proven by tests that bypass the service and issue raw Supabase `.update()`/`.delete()` calls directly against published/terminal rows.

**Deliberately deferred, named rather than built** (both read as "Do NOT redesign Achievement/Portfolio" from this sprint's own mission, not a shortcut): Achievement's `competition_id` reference column (ADR-0014 Phase 3's "Achievement references Competition, going forward") was not added — `learner_achievements`' schema was not touched at all; the reference point is satisfied by `learner_competitions.id` being a real, stable primary key a future, Achievement-domain-led migration can point at. Portfolio's read-surface for Competitions was likewise not built — Portfolio's category taxonomy has no `competitions` slot at all (permanently reassigned to Achievement by ADR-0012), so there is nothing for a link column to attach to; `lib/learnerPortfolio/` was not touched.

**Tests**: `lib/learnerCompetitions/competition.integration.test.ts` — 12 tests against real synthetic Supabase data, all passing: full lifecycle (all nine main-line states, versioning, ordered history), all three terminal branches individually, canonical level/category enforcement, Blueprint composition (unavailable/available/currentParticipation shape, judging/feedback text asserted absent from the serialized section), the Competition→Project reference invariant (re-reads the Project row after linking, proves it untouched), team membership, cross-school isolation, permission checks, repository method-shape reflection, and — per the mission's explicit instruction — a regression test that builds a real Achievement, Portfolio item, and Project in the same fixture as a Competition, drives each through its own lifecycle, and asserts all three summaries plus the full `composeBlueprint()` output are correct and mutually undisturbed. The two Blueprint test fixtures (`composeBlueprint.pure.test.ts`, `growthTimeline.pure.test.ts`) needed the same one-line `competitions: na(...)` addition every prior domain addition required (the closed-type discipline Sprint 12AA already documented as expected). Full existing `lib/**/*.pure.test.ts` suite (34 tests) and the Sprint 12AB architecture test (4 tests) re-run and pass unaffected.

**Verification performed**: `npx tsc --noEmit -p .` clean; `npx eslint` clean (0 errors) on every new/changed file; migration applied only after explicit user approval via `AskUserQuestion`.

**Explicitly out of scope, left unbuilt per the Stop Condition**: Competition UI, uploads, certificate rendering, event catalog, judge portal, parent portal integration, QR, analytics, dashboards, rankings, notifications, AI recommendations. `moveToHistorical()` exists and is tested but nothing calls it on a time-based schedule — no cron was wired.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0014-learner-competitions-domain.md` (the ADR this sprint implements in full), `docs/architecture/sprint-13a-learner-competitions-architecture.md`, ADR-0012/0013 (Achievement/Projects immutability and reference-not-copy precedent this sprint's schema/trigger/service directly mirrors).

**ADR**: No new ADR and no change to ADR-0014 — implementation matched the frozen architecture exactly; no point required stopping to document an ADR conflict.

**Tests added**: `lib/learnerCompetitions/competition.integration.test.ts` (12 tests, all passing). Two pre-existing Blueprint test fixtures updated (one-line additive fix each).

**Rollback considerations**: Low-to-moderate. The migration is additive (four new tables, no existing table altered, no data migration) — dropping the four new tables and their triggers/policies would fully revert the database side with no impact on any other domain's data. Code-side: `lib/repositories/competition.repository.ts`, `lib/learnerCompetitions/`, and `lib/learnerBlueprint/composeCompetitions.ts` are net-new files with no other code depending on them yet (Competitions has no live UI/route consumer this sprint); reverting the four wiring edits to `composeBlueprint.ts`/`types.ts`/`validation.ts`/`repositories/index.ts` fully removes Competitions from the Blueprint composition path.

## 2026-07-19 — Sprint 13C: Learner Leadership Domain (Architecture Only)

**What changed**: nothing executable — an architecture-only sprint under Guardian Architect Mode, the fourth in this domain-freeze series (after Achievement, Projects, Competitions). Produced `docs/architecture/adr-0015-learner-leadership-domain.md` and `docs/architecture/sprint-13c-learner-leadership-architecture.md`. No table, migration, repository, service, route, UI, election, voting, or messaging mechanism was written; Achievement, Portfolio, Projects, Competitions, Blueprint, Parent Experience, and Career Intelligence were read only, for ownership verification, never modified.

**Audit finding (Phase 1, mandatory, done first)**: searched the entire codebase for every leadership-related term named in the mission and read every plausible hit's actual implementation. Found exactly one real, partial prior implementation — `AchievementType`/`AchievementCategory` already include `'leadership'` (`lib/repositories/achievement.repository.ts`, ADR-0012), a flat, after-the-fact claim carrying only `title`/`description`/`awardingOrganization`/`awardDate` — despite ADR-0012 Phase 2's own text describing Leadership as carrying "a role title, scope, and duration," none of which the shipped schema stores. The identical situation ADR-0014 already found for Competitions. `ProjectCategory` also includes `'leadership'` — confirmed as the *work* a leader does, not the responsibility itself, no change needed. No canonical Leadership domain, no rival implementations, no naming collision beyond the already-resolved Portfolio/Achievement word overlap.

**Architecture frozen in ADR-0015**: a new canonical domain, Learner Leadership, owns demonstrated responsibility held over time (Nomination → Selection → Active Service → Review → Completion → Verification → Published → Historical, plus four named terminal branches — Not Selected, Discontinued, Rejected, Revoked). The mission's central distinction — leadership *recognition* (Achievement-owned, a discrete award) versus leadership *service* (Leadership-owned, the ongoing responsibility) — is frozen explicitly in Phase 7, with neither implying the other. Achievement's `leadership` claim type is not removed or redesigned; it gains an optional reference field to a Leadership Entry going forward, the same pattern already used for Achievement's `competition` type. Portfolio references Leadership one direction only, with an honest note that Portfolio has no `leadership` category slot today (reassigned to Achievement by ADR-0012) — deferred to a future Portfolio-led decision, not built here. Career may read verified Leadership as evidence but never computes employability. Elections/voting are explicitly rejected as a Leadership-owned concept — the domain records only the fact of who was selected, never runs or tallies anything, directly protecting against popularity-bias risk. A "Discontinued" exit state exists for a role ending early but carries only a neutral factual note, never a disciplinary case record — disciplinary process is explicitly named as a separate, not-yet-designed domain Leadership must never become a substitute for.

**Deliberately deferred, named rather than silently assumed**: a shared Position/title catalog (to prevent title inflation and duplicate re-typing) is reserved as a future extension, not built speculatively; the exact verifying-party data model; how Achievement's `leadership` field evolves to reference a real Leadership Entry; and nine named future leadership sub-types (Student Parliament, House Leadership, Club Executive, Community Leadership, Faith Leadership, Sports Leadership, Digital Leadership, Peer Mentorship, Alumni Leadership) — reserved only, no schema, no premature taxonomy commitment.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0014-learner-competitions-domain.md` (the most directly analogous prior ADR — same audit method, same lifecycle-freeze discipline applied one domain earlier), ADR-0005 through ADR-0010 (Blueprint/Parent Experience series), ADR-0011 (Portfolio, its original provisional Leadership row), ADR-0012 (Achievement, the `leadership` type this ADR disambiguates), ADR-0013 (Projects, reference-not-copy precedent), `reference-architecture-specification.md` §3, Educational Constitution.

**ADR**: `docs/architecture/adr-0015-learner-leadership-domain.md` — Status: DRAFT, awaiting explicit approval before Sprint 13D (the first Learner Leadership implementation sprint). Partially supersedes ADR-0012 Phase 2's `leadership` AchievementType entry (adds a reference field; does not remove or rename it). Does not supersede any other ADR.

**Tests added**: None (architecture-only, no code changed).

**Rollback considerations**: None — no code, schema, or data was touched; both new files are additive documentation.

## 2026-07-20 — Sprint 13E: Learner Community Service Domain (Architecture Only)

**What changed**: nothing executable — an architecture-only sprint under Guardian Architect Mode, the fifth in this domain-freeze series (after Achievement, Projects, Competitions, Leadership). Produced `docs/architecture/adr-0016-learner-community-service-domain.md` and `docs/architecture/sprint-13e-community-service-architecture.md`. No table, migration, repository, service, route, UI, or upload mechanism was written; Achievement, Portfolio, Projects, Competitions, Leadership, Blueprint, and Career Intelligence were read only, for ownership verification, never modified.

**Audit finding (Phase 1, mandatory, done first)**: searched the entire codebase for every community-service-related term named in the mission and read every plausible hit's actual implementation. Found exactly one real, partial prior implementation — `AchievementType`/`AchievementCategory` already include `'community_service'` (ADR-0012), a flat, after-the-fact claim carrying only `title`/`description`/`awardingOrganization`/`awardDate` — despite ADR-0012 Phase 2's own text describing it as carrying "hours, activity type, verifying party," none of which the shipped schema stores. The identical situation already found twice before for Competitions and Leadership. `ProjectCategory` also includes `'community'`/`'environmental'` — confirmed as the *work*, not the ongoing service relationship, no change needed. One infrastructure naming collision found and dismissed: `lib/environment/` is the platform's own runtime-config module, unrelated to environmental service.

**Architecture frozen in ADR-0016**: a new canonical domain, Learner Community Service, owns verified, sustained contribution rendered over time (Opportunity → Commitment → Active Service → Review → Completion → Verification → Published → Historical, plus three named terminal branches — Discontinued, Rejected, Revoked). Deliberately shaped like Leadership's lifecycle (an ongoing relationship) rather than Competitions' (a bounded event), per the mission's own "reflect service over time, not merely participation" instruction. A fourth possible terminal branch ("Not Undertaken," mirroring Leadership's Not Selected) was explicitly considered and rejected with reasoning: an untaken service Opportunity has no third-party decision worth preserving as a distinct fact, unlike a Nomination. Six Educational Principles were frozen — the five the mission named plus one more ("compulsory service is recorded honestly, never inflated to appear voluntary"), justified directly by the Phase 8 "compulsory-service inflation" risk it prevents, not invented speculatively. All ten cross-domain relationships the mission named were frozen one-directionally; most notably, Leadership gets **no relationship to Community Service at all** — the specific architectural protection against double-counting the mission asked to be addressed. Achievement's existing `community_service` claim type is not removed or redesigned; it gains an optional reference field to a Community Service Entry going forward, the same pattern already used for `competition`/`leadership`.

**Deliberately deferred, named rather than silently assumed**: a shared Cause/Organization catalog is reserved as a future extension, not built speculatively; the exact verifying-party data model; how Achievement's `community_service` field evolves to reference a real Entry; the exact voluntary/compulsory flag's data model; and a possible future public-facing surface (Phase 7's "Public" visibility column, explicitly reserved, not decided).

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0015-learner-leadership-domain.md` (the most directly analogous prior ADR — the ongoing-relationship lifecycle shape this ADR reapplies), `docs/architecture/adr-0014-learner-competitions-domain.md` (the "no separate catalog"/"automatic transition"/terminal-branch-justification discipline), ADR-0005 through ADR-0010 (Blueprint/Parent Experience series), ADR-0011 (Portfolio, its original provisional Community Service row), ADR-0012 (Achievement, the `community_service` type this ADR disambiguates, including its own Citizenship/Environmental folding decision), ADR-0013 (Projects, reference-not-copy precedent), `reference-architecture-specification.md` §3, Educational Constitution.

**ADR**: `docs/architecture/adr-0016-learner-community-service-domain.md` — Status: DRAFT, awaiting explicit approval before Sprint 13F (the first Learner Community Service implementation sprint). Partially supersedes ADR-0012 Phase 2's `community_service` AchievementType entry (adds a reference field; does not remove or rename it). Does not supersede any other ADR.

**Tests added**: None (architecture-only, no code changed).

**Rollback considerations**: None — no code, schema, or data was touched; both new files are additive documentation.

## 2026-07-20 — Sprint 13F: Learner Wellbeing Domain (Architecture Only, Guardian Mode)

**What changed**: nothing executable — an architecture-only sprint under Guardian Architect Mode, the sixth in this domain-freeze series and the first built almost entirely on the *absence* of relationships to its siblings, given the sensitivity of the subject matter. Produced `docs/architecture/adr-0017-learner-wellbeing-domain.md` and `docs/architecture/sprint-13f-learner-wellbeing-architecture.md`. No table, migration, repository, service, route, API, React component, UI, portal, dashboard, notification, evidence ingestion, AI summarizer, wellbeing scoring, or recommendation engine was written; every sibling domain (Teacher Reflection, Portfolio, Achievement, Projects, Competitions, Leadership, Community Service, Blueprint, Parent Experience, Career Intelligence, Learning Compass, Attendance, Evidence) was read only, for ownership verification, never modified.

**Audit finding (Phase 1, mandatory, done first, no assumption that Wellbeing does not exist)**: searched the entire codebase for every wellbeing-related term named in the mission. Zero hits for "wellbeing"/"well-being"/"well being" in any spelling, anywhere. The single most important finding: Teacher Reflection's own migration and service (Sprint 12O) already explicitly and permanently exclude "behaviour, discipline, counselling" and "diagnosis, predictions, personality typing, medical claims" from their own scope — a real, prior architectural decision that this gap was recognized once already and correctly left unfilled rather than improvised. Also confirmed the Projection Engine's `RiskFlag`/`RiskValue` and related "concern" vocabulary hits are purely academic risk (declining subject performance), never psychological — a vocabulary-adjacent, not overlapping, system, named explicitly so it's never confused with a wellbeing concern.

**Architecture frozen in ADR-0017**: a new canonical domain, Learner Wellbeing, owns the confidential record that support was needed, provided, and closed — never a diagnosis, a score, or a judgment about conduct. Fourteen ownership-matrix concepts frozen, including a deliberate two-tier Plan/Check-in structure so minor needs aren't over-formalized. Nine concepts explicitly rejected (diagnosis, psychology engine, medical record, discipline system, attendance replacement, teacher-commentary replacement, AI emotional detector, surveillance system, behaviour/risk score), each with its own stated reason. The lifecycle deliberately has **no Published state** — the single biggest structural departure from every prior domain in this series, reasoned explicitly rather than copied: Wellbeing produces no external, showcaseable credential, so ending in "Closed" (permanently confidential) rather than "Published" is the correct, deliberate design. Ten constitutional constraints frozen, most importantly permanent separation from any future discipline/Behaviour domain (zero relationship, forever) and an absolute "no numeric score, ever" rule. Of thirteen possible cross-domain relationships, **eleven are explicitly "none"** — including, uniquely in this series, Blueprint and Parent Experience, both deliberately excluded from their otherwise-universal patterns given the domain's privacy stakes; only two narrow, read-only references exist (Attendance, Evidence). A closed set of visibility tiers was frozen, departing from every sibling domain's blanket school-staff-read RLS pattern in favor of a Support-Team-scoped model — the first domain in this series to require a stricter-than-standard security model, justified explicitly. An exhaustive AI "may"/"must never" boundary was frozen, requiring its own separately-approved ADR to ever expand.

**Rejected alternatives, documented explicitly** (per the mission's own instruction): copying the standard Published-ending lifecycle shape; naming the first state "Opportunity"; a single-tier (no Check-in) Plan model; the standard blanket school-staff RLS pattern; wiring even a bare existence flag into Blueprint; letting Parent Experience's default read pattern apply here.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0006-blueprint-educational-experience.md` §6 (Teacher Reflection's own frozen exclusion — direct precedent this ADR formalizes), `docs/architecture/adr-0010-parent-experience-architecture.md` (the default pattern this ADR deliberately departs from), ADR-0011 through ADR-0016 (the full canonical-domain series, read for what not to duplicate and for the "no relationship, named explicitly" protection pattern), `reference-architecture-specification.md` §3/§8, Educational Constitution.

**ADR**: `docs/architecture/adr-0017-learner-wellbeing-domain.md` — Status: DRAFT, awaiting explicit approval before Sprint 13G (the first Learner Wellbeing implementation sprint). Supersedes nothing — no prior ADR ever provisionally assigned Wellbeing to another domain.

**Tests added**: None (architecture-only, no code changed).

**Rollback considerations**: None — no code, schema, or data was touched; both new files are additive documentation.

## 2026-07-21 — Sprint 13G: Learner Wellbeing Foundation (Canonical Domain Implementation)

**What changed**: implemented ADR-0017 in full — the canonical Learner Wellbeing domain now exists as a secure, standalone, confidential domain with deliberately minimal architectural coupling. Full findings and phase-by-phase record in `docs/architecture/sprint-13g-learner-wellbeing-foundation.md`.

**Re-audit (Phase 1, mandatory, done first)**: re-swept the repository for every wellbeing-related term and re-confirmed no implementation, duplicate repository, counselling module, or hidden table existed anywhere; nothing shipped since Sprint 13F changed the plan.

**What was built**:
- `supabase/migrations/20260721090000_learner_wellbeing.sql` — additive only, applied to the live project after explicit user approval. **Three tables, not the two named as an example** — `learner_wellbeing_cases`, `wellbeing_support_team` (the real access-control unit, a necessary consequence of implementing the mission's own Support-Team-scoped security mandate, not a speculative extra), and `learner_wellbeing_updates` (a unified append-only stream combining lifecycle-transition history with qualitative content). Eight statuses matching ADR-0017 Phase 5 exactly — six main-line states (concern_raised→initial_assessment→support_plan_active→review→outcome_recorded→closed) plus two terminal branches (no_action_needed, withdrawn) — and, critically, **no Verification state and no Published state**, ADR-0017's deliberate, central departure from every prior domain in this series.
- `lib/repositories/wellbeing.repository.ts` — `WellbeingRepository`, registered as `repos.wellbeing`. One named method per transition; no generic update/delete/mutate; no `publish()` method exists at all.
- `lib/learnerWellbeing/{types,validation,wellbeing}.ts` — the canonical service. Defines its own, stricter-than-every-sibling authorization gate, `requireSupportTeamMembership()`, layered on top of ordinary school membership — the only domain in this series whose access control is not `requireSchoolStaff` for read/write actions on an existing record. A two-tier visibility model (`core_team`/`school_leadership`) is enforced twice: once via RLS, once again in the service layer (since the repository itself performs no filtering, per its own "persistence only" discipline).
- `lib/learnerWellbeing/wellbeingBoundary.architecture.test.ts` — 7 static tests proving, by walking the real source tree, that Blueprint, Parent Experience, and any (nonexistent) Behaviour module have zero coupling to Wellbeing, in either direction, and that no "Wellbeing Status"/"Support Needed"/"Risk Level" string exists anywhere in Blueprint's own code.
- Immutability enforced at all three required layers, proven against the raw service-role client directly (not just the anon/authenticated path) — `enforce_wellbeing_case_immutability()` has no "published→archived/revoked" carve-out the way every sibling domain's trigger does, since Closed is genuinely terminal with nothing past it.

**Deliberately not built, per the Stop Condition**: any UI, portal, dashboard, notification, email/SMS, AI summary, diagnosis field, behaviour/discipline tracking, emotional/wellbeing/risk score, prediction engine, or integration of any kind with Blueprint, Parent Experience, Portfolio, Achievement, Projects, Competitions, Leadership, or Community Service.

**Tests**: `lib/learnerWellbeing/wellbeing.integration.test.ts` — 10 tests against real synthetic Supabase data: full lifecycle (asserting no verifiedBy/publishedAt field exists anywhere on a closed case), both terminal branches, Escalation Status's independence from the main lifecycle, Support-Team-scoped access proven at both the service layer AND via a raw RLS-gated read returning zero rows for a real, active, non-team teacher at the same school, visibility-tier filtering (a school_leadership-role member proven never to see core_team-classified content, and the "tighten only" rule proven to reject a loosening attempt), parent/learner denial at the very first gate, cross-school isolation, repository method-shape reflection, and — per the mission's explicit Phase 7 instruction, proven with live data rather than only statically — `composeBlueprint()`'s full output asserted byte-for-byte identical before and after a Wellbeing case exists for the same learner. All 17 new tests (10 integration + 7 boundary) pass. Full existing `lib/**/*.pure.test.ts` suite (34 tests) and the Sprint 12AB architecture test (4 tests) re-run and pass unaffected — no Blueprint fixture needed any change this sprint, since `LearnerBlueprint`'s type was never touched (the first domain sprint in this series where that's true).

**Verification performed**: `npx tsc --noEmit -p .` clean; `npx eslint` clean (0 errors) on every new/changed file; migration applied only after explicit user approval via `AskUserQuestion`.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0017-learner-wellbeing-domain.md` (the ADR this sprint implements in full), `docs/architecture/sprint-13f-learner-wellbeing-architecture.md`, ADR-0006 §6 (Teacher Reflection's validation-limitation precedent, reused verbatim in this domain's own validation module's header), ADR-0012/0013/0014/0015/0016 (the three-layer immutability discipline this sprint's schema/trigger/service directly mirrors, minus the Published-state carve-out, which does not apply here).

**ADR**: No new ADR and no change to ADR-0017 — implementation matched the frozen architecture exactly; no point required stopping to document an ADR conflict.

**Tests added**: `lib/learnerWellbeing/wellbeing.integration.test.ts` (10 tests) and `lib/learnerWellbeing/wellbeingBoundary.architecture.test.ts` (7 tests) — 17 total.

**Rollback considerations**: Low-to-moderate. The migration is additive (three new tables, no existing table altered, no data migration) — dropping the three new tables and their triggers/policies would fully revert the database side with zero impact on any other domain's data, since this domain has no relationship to any of them. Code-side: `lib/repositories/wellbeing.repository.ts` and `lib/learnerWellbeing/` are net-new files with no other code depending on them (Wellbeing has no live UI/route consumer, and — uniquely in this series — no Blueprint wiring to revert either); the one edit to `lib/repositories/index.ts` (registering `repos.wellbeing`) is trivially reversible.

## 2026-07-21 — Sprint 13H: Learner Innovation Domain (Architecture Only)

**What changed**: nothing executable — an architecture-only sprint under Guardian Architect Mode, the seventh in this domain-freeze series (after Achievement, Projects, Competitions, Leadership, Community Service, Wellbeing). Produced `docs/architecture/adr-0018-learner-innovation-domain.md` and `docs/architecture/sprint-13h-learner-innovation-architecture.md`. No table, migration, repository, service, route, UI, upload mechanism, prototype storage, or AI evaluation was written; Portfolio, Achievement, Projects, Competitions, Career Intelligence, Blueprint, and Learning Compass were read only, for ownership verification, never modified.

**Audit finding (Phase 1, mandatory, done first)**: searched the entire codebase for every innovation-related term named in the mission. This is the first ADR in the series to find the same word as a bare classification label in **three** sibling domains at once — `AchievementType`/`AchievementCategory` include `'innovation'` (a flat recognition claim), `ProjectCategory` includes `'innovation'` (a work-classification label), and `CompetitionCategory` includes `'innovation'` (an event-classification label) — none of the three tracks the actual evolutionary process. Portfolio's original provisional "Innovation" row (ADR-0011) was already superseded by ADR-0012 before Portfolio's first sprint.

**Architecture frozen in ADR-0018**: the core educational question — "what makes something an Innovation rather than merely a Project, Achievement, or Competition" — was answered with a falsifiable, evidence-checkable test rather than a motivational one: **demonstrated iteration**. A single polished, unchanged final product is evidence of execution, not innovation; a documented trail of problem→idea→prototype→test→refinement is. A new canonical domain, Learner Innovation, owns exactly that evolution (Idea → Exploration → Prototype → Testing → Refinement → Validation → Implementation → Archived, plus three justified terminal branches — Discontinued, Not Validated, Revoked), deliberately not copied from any sibling domain's lifecycle shape. Repeated Prototype/Testing/Refinement cycles are modeled as append-only Iteration-log entries at a fixed status, never as the main lifecycle looping backward — preserving every sibling domain's forward-only discipline. No separate Verification/Published pair was added on top of Validation/Implementation, reasoned explicitly as unnecessary bureaucracy rather than an oversight. All eleven named cross-domain relationships were frozen one-directionally; Innovation follows the *standard* Blueprint/Parent-Experience read pattern (unlike Wellbeing's deliberate departure, since Innovation is a showcase-type domain) but gets zero relationship to Wellbeing, Community Service, and Leadership — the same "no relationship, named explicitly" double-counting protection ADR-0016/0017 already established. Nine educational principles were frozen (the mission's eight, plus one more — "no score, no ranking, ever" — justified directly by the popularity-bias risk it prevents).

**Deliberately deferred, named rather than silently assumed**: the exact Iteration-log entry-type vocabulary, the exact Mentor data model, how Achievement's `innovation` field evolves to reference a real Innovation Entry, and fifteen named future extensions (Research, Patents, Open-source work, Hackathons, Maker spaces, University collaborations, etc.) — reserved only, with Patents/Industry mentorship/University collaborations flagged as likely needing their own future ADR given legal/institutional stakes.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0016-learner-community-service-domain.md`/`adr-0017-learner-wellbeing-domain.md` (the "no relationship, named explicitly" protection pattern, reused here), `adr-0014-learner-competitions-domain.md` (terminal-branch-justification discipline), ADR-0011/0012/0013 (the `innovation` classification values across Portfolio/Achievement/Projects this ADR disambiguates), `reference-architecture-specification.md` §3, Educational Constitution.

**ADR**: `docs/architecture/adr-0018-learner-innovation-domain.md` — Status: DRAFT, awaiting explicit approval before Sprint 13I (the first Learner Innovation implementation sprint). Partially supersedes ADR-0012 Phase 2's `innovation` AchievementType entry (adds a reference field; does not remove or rename it). Does not supersede any other ADR.

**Tests added**: None (architecture-only, no code changed).

**Rollback considerations**: None — no code, schema, or data was touched; both new files are additive documentation.

## 2026-07-22 — Sprint 13I: Learner Innovation Foundation (Canonical Domain Implementation)

**What changed**: implemented ADR-0018 in full — the canonical Learner Innovation domain now exists. Full findings and phase-by-phase record in `docs/architecture/sprint-13i-learner-innovation-foundation.md`.

**Re-audit (Phase 1, mandatory, done first)**: re-swept the repository and re-read Portfolio/Projects/Achievement/Competitions/Blueprint/Parent Experience before writing any code. Confirmed no Innovation repository, service, schema, or route existed; "Innovation" existed only as a bare classification value in three sibling domains (Achievement, Projects, Competitions), none of which could safely be reused.

**What was built**:
- `supabase/migrations/20260722090000_learner_innovation.sql` — additive only, applied to the live project after explicit user approval. Four tables matching the mission's own suggested names exactly: `learner_innovations` (the lifecycle-bearing entity — eleven statuses: eight main-line, Idea→Exploration→Prototype→Testing→Refinement→Validation→Implementation→Archived, plus three terminal branches — Discontinued, Not Validated, Revoked), `innovation_iterations` (the domain's unique architectural feature — Problem/Hypothesis/Change introduced/Evidence/Outcome/Teacher note, append-only and immutable from the moment of creation, unconditionally, regardless of the parent record's status), `innovation_artifacts` (link-out media only, mirroring `achievement_media`/`competition_media`), `innovation_review_history` (the lifecycle-transition audit trail every sibling domain's own `*_history` table already establishes). Required properties all present: forward-only lifecycle, append-only iteration history, immutable published records, teacher verification, school ownership, `created_at`/`updated_at`/`published_at`/`archived_at`/`schema_version`. No scoring, ranking, popularity metric, or AI field exists anywhere in the schema.
- `lib/repositories/innovation.repository.ts` — `InnovationRepository`, registered as `repos.innovations`. One named method per lifecycle transition (`createIdea`, `beginExploration`, `createPrototype`, `moveToTesting`, `moveToRefinement`, `validateInnovation`, `markNotValidated`, `implementInnovation`, `archiveInnovation`, `revokeInnovation`, `discontinueInnovation`), matching the mission's own suggested method-name pattern. No generic `update()`/`delete()`/`mutate()`/`save()` — proven by a reflection-based test.
- `lib/learnerInnovation/{types,validation,innovation}.ts` — the canonical service. Owns lifecycle enforcement, authorization, iteration creation, and publication rules only; zero imports of Blueprint/Portfolio/Achievement/Projects/Career/Parent Experience/Learning Compass (grep-verified). `validateInnovation()`/`markNotValidated()` are modeled as two sibling outcomes of the same gate (both reachable from `refinement`), a reasoned implementation clarification documented in the module's own header, mirroring Achievement's verify/reject and Leadership's publish/reject pairs.
- `lib/learnerBlueprint/composeInnovation.ts`, wired into `composeBlueprint.ts`/`types.ts`/`validation.ts` via the identical 5-file pattern this series now uses. Returns exactly six fields (`currentStage`, `iterationCount`, `latestMilestone`, `latestImplementationDate`, `innovationsUrl`, plus availability) — iteration history/teacher notes/internal review/artifacts/testing data are structurally absent from the type, not filtered at runtime.
- Immutability enforced at all three required layers — `enforce_innovation_immutability()` mirrors every sibling domain's identical trigger discipline (`implementation` allows exactly two further transitions, to `archived` or `revoked`; every other terminal status is fully immutable; delete legal only in the earliest state); `innovation_iterations`/`innovation_review_history` are unconditionally append-only from creation, proven both mid-lifecycle and post-archival in the same test.

**Deliberately deferred, named rather than built**: Achievement's `innovation_id` reference column (ADR-0018 Phase 6's "Achievement references Innovation, going forward") was not added — `learner_achievements`' schema was not touched at all, mirroring every prior domain's identical deferral pattern.

**Tests**: `lib/learnerInnovation/innovation.integration.test.ts` — 10 tests against real synthetic Supabase data: full lifecycle (all eight main-line states, versioning, ordered review history, append-only iteration logging with an ordering proof), validation-required gating (implementation attempted directly from refinement rejected), all three terminal branches individually (Discontinued specifically requiring both a reason and a lessons-learned field per "failure is educational evidence"), Blueprint composition (unavailable/available/currentStage shape, iteration-content text asserted absent from the serialized summary), cross-school isolation, permission checks, repository method-shape reflection, and — per the mission's explicit Phase 10 instruction — a live regression test driving Achievement, Portfolio, Projects, Competitions, and Leadership each through a real lifecycle in the same fixture as an Innovation entry, asserting every sibling summary function and the full `composeBlueprint()` output remain correct and mutually undisturbed. `lib/learnerInnovation/innovationBoundary.architecture.test.ts` — 5 static tests proving zero coupling to Blueprint/Portfolio/Achievement/Projects/Career/Parent Experience/Learning Compass by walking the real source tree. All 15 new tests pass. Full existing `lib/**/*.pure.test.ts` suite (34 tests) and the Sprint 12AB architecture test (4 tests) re-run and pass unaffected.

**Verification performed**: `npx tsc --noEmit -p .` clean; `npx eslint` clean (0 errors) on every new/changed file; migration applied only after explicit user approval via `AskUserQuestion`.

**Explicitly out of scope, left unbuilt per the Stop Condition**: UI, uploads interface, AI evaluation, innovation score, rankings, popularity metrics, and any redesign of Portfolio/Achievement/Competition/Project/Parent Portal/Career Intelligence/QR/Evidence/Blueprint. `archiveInnovation()` exists and is tested but nothing calls it on a time-based schedule — no cron was wired.

**Architectural documents referenced**: `docs/architecture/adr-0018-learner-innovation-domain.md` (the ADR this sprint implements in full), `docs/architecture/sprint-13h-learner-innovation-architecture.md`, ADR-0012/0013/0014/0015 (the three-layer immutability discipline and terminal-branch-justification pattern this sprint's schema/trigger/service directly mirrors).

**ADR**: No new ADR and no change to ADR-0018 — implementation matched the frozen architecture exactly, with one reasoned implementation clarification (the Validation/Not-Validated gate shape) documented in code rather than requiring an ADR amendment.

**Tests added**: `lib/learnerInnovation/innovation.integration.test.ts` (10 tests) and `lib/learnerInnovation/innovationBoundary.architecture.test.ts` (5 tests) — 15 total. Two pre-existing Blueprint test fixtures updated (one-line additive fix each).

**Rollback considerations**: Low-to-moderate. The migration is additive (four new tables, no existing table altered, no data migration) — dropping the four new tables and their triggers/policies would fully revert the database side with no impact on any other domain's data. Code-side: `lib/repositories/innovation.repository.ts`, `lib/learnerInnovation/`, and `lib/learnerBlueprint/composeInnovation.ts` are net-new files with no other code depending on them yet (Innovation has no live UI/route consumer this sprint); reverting the four wiring edits to `composeBlueprint.ts`/`types.ts`/`validation.ts`/`repositories/index.ts` fully removes Innovation from the Blueprint composition path.
